# M3 — Desktop Library Explorer Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The desktop app opens a ROM library (dialog, drag-and-drop or a recent entry), scans it through `coverly-core` with progress, and lets the user browse 10k+ ROMs smoothly: Folders / Systems / Date / All views, a virtualised cover grid and a table, combinable filters and accent-insensitive search with live counts, keyboard navigation, and a detail drawer with art previews, file info, on-demand identification and "Reveal in Finder". In a plain browser (`pnpm dev`) the same UI runs against a deterministic mock backend, so it can be tested end to end with Chrome DevTools MCP.

**Architecture:** `src-tauri` gains a thin, testable library layer (`error`, `library::{view, art, identify}`, `state`) over `coverly-core` (`scan_library_with_progress`, `resolve_art`, `fit_to_root`, `settings`, `Identifier`). The backend keeps the opened library as a `Session` (generation number + canonical root + per-entry paths); the webview only ever sees a compact serialisable `LibraryView` and asks for art, identification and reveal **by entry id**, never by path. The frontend talks to one `Backend` interface with two adapters — Tauri (`invoke`/`listen`) and a browser mock backed by a seeded fixture generator — and keeps state in small zustand stores (library, view, filters, selection, identity, locale); all filtering, faceting, sorting and tree building are pure functions.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 1.90 (edition 2021), Tauri 2.12, new crates `tauri-plugin-dialog ~2.8` (same major.minor as the npm `@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog` 2.8.x already in `pnpm-lock.yaml`: the Tauri CLI refuses mismatched plugin versions), `tauri-plugin-opener 2` (Rust only, no npm package), `thiserror` (workspace) and `tempfile` (dev). React 19 + TypeScript 6 (strict, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`) + Tailwind 3 + zustand 5, new dependency `@tanstack/react-virtual@^3.13`; existing `@tauri-apps/api` 2.12 and `@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog` 2.8. Tests: `cargo test` (unit + integration in `src-tauri/tests`), Vitest 5 + Testing Library + jsdom. E2E: Chrome DevTools MCP against `pnpm dev`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-coverly-roms-design.md` — §5 (domain), §6 (architecture: core never depends on Tauri, app is thin, long work on worker threads with progress), §7 steps 1–3, **§8 UI (Start, Library, Filters)**, §9 "Library" settings (ignored folders, recent libraries), §10, §11, §12, §15 (M3 exit: "Browse the real SD smoothly (≥ 10k entries)") and §16 (refinements win on conflict). Tracker rows M3-01..M3-05 and decisions D1–D15 in `docs/TRACKER.md`. UI/IPC/store/test patterns: Pluryprint (`/Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/pluryprint`, read-only reference: `src/lib/ipc.ts`, `src/lib/ipcError.ts`, `src/stores/*.ts`, `src-tauri/src/ipc/diag.rs` for the opener plugin used from Rust only).

## Global Constraints

- Work on branch `feat/m3-explorer`; never switch branches, never push, never touch `main`. Every shell command starts with `cd /Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/coverly-roms &&`.
- TDD: write the failing test, run it and see it fail, implement, see it pass.
- After **every** task run all gates and fix anything red before committing: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm format:check`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm build`.
- Commits: conventional, granular, English. **Never** add `Co-Authored-By` or any Claude attribution. Do not push.
- `rust-version = "1.90"` and `edition = "2021"` stay. If a crate version needs a newer compiler, pin the newest one whose `rust-version` is ≤ 1.90.
- The core never depends on Tauri and holds no UI strings (spec §6). `src-tauri` stays thin: commands are one-liners over functions in `src-tauri/src/{library,state}` that are unit-testable without a Tauri runtime.
- Tests never hit the real network: Rust uses fake `Http` implementations; the UI uses the mock backend or `mockIPC`.
- Real ROM data for manual/E2E checks: `/Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/ROMS` (55 folders, 7,473 ROMs) — **strictly read-only**. Never create, modify or delete anything under `/Volumes`. Any write test uses a COPY under `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-miguelangelesparzacalero-Documents-GitHub/35b7c020-f2ae-412f-b757-99c24a9a498b/scratchpad`.
- Every UI string goes through i18n (`locales/en/ui.json` **and** `locales/es/ui.json`, same keys; a parity test enforces it).
- File names are hostile: render them as React text only (never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, never `innerHTML`), escape them when building SVG/XML, and show long names truncated with the full name in `title`.
- The webview can read art **only** inside the opened library root: art is requested by `(generation, entry_id, media)`, the backend resolves the path itself, canonicalises it and refuses anything outside the canonical root (symlink escape) or larger than 20 MiB. The Tauri asset protocol stays disabled; no `fs` plugin; the opener plugin is used from Rust only.
- Tauri command arguments are camelCase on the JS side (`entryId`, `profileId`, `ignoredDirs`) and snake_case in Rust; wire payloads (`LibraryView`…) keep Rust's snake_case field names.
- Errors cross IPC as `{ "code": "<kebab-case>", "detail"?: … }` and are shown with the i18n key `errors.<code>`.
- Performance budget: a 12k-entry library mounts at most 120 grid tiles / 60 table rows at any time; opening a generated 12k tree takes < 20 s in a debug test build (release target < 2 s, measured in Task 13).

## Review Focus

1. **Art reads that escape the library** — an `Imgs/<stem>.png` that is a symlink to `/etc/hosts`, an `Imgs` folder symlinked outside the root, an unknown entry id or an id from a library that was closed/reopened must never return bytes: `outside-library`, `unknown-entry`, `stale-library`. Tests: Task 4 (`symlinked_art_outside_the_library_is_refused`, `symlinked_art_folder_outside_is_refused`, `unknown_entry_is_rejected`, `stale_generation_is_rejected`).
2. **Hostile file names** — `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>.sfc`, a right-to-left override, `..\..\escape`, a 180-character name: shown as literal text in grid, table and drawer, escaped inside mock SVG art, never creating elements. Tests: Task 3 (`hostile_names_are_kept_verbatim`), Task 7 (`mock_art_escapes_hostile_names`), Task 10 (`renders_hostile_names_as_text`), Task 12 (`drawer_renders_hostile_name_as_text`).
3. **Opening a second library while the first still scans** (or double-clicking a recent) — the older result must never replace the newer one, its progress must not leak into the new progress bar, and art/identify calls for the old generation are rejected. Tests: Task 4 (`a_newer_open_supersedes_an_older_one`), Task 9 (`ignores_a_stale_open_result`).
4. **Picking the "wrong" folder** — the ROMS folder itself instead of the SD root, an empty folder, a folder with no recognisable layout, a removed/ejected recent library: the user gets the library or a clear translated message, never a blank screen or 0 ROMs without explanation. Tests: Task 1 (`picking_the_roms_folder_itself_scans_it_directly`), Task 3 (`opens_the_roms_folder_itself`, `folder_without_layout_is_no_profile`), Task 9 (`shows_a_translated_error_when_opening_fails`, `ejected_recent_is_disabled`), Task 11 (`empty_library_explains_itself`).
5. **Broken settings file** — a corrupt or unwritable `library.json` must fall back to defaults and never block opening a library. Tests: Task 2 (`corrupt_file_falls_back_to_defaults_and_is_kept_aside`), Task 4 (`open_succeeds_when_settings_cannot_be_saved`).

## Deviations from the spec / task brief (decided in this plan)

- **Folders tree is derived in the UI** from each entry's `dir` (`buildFolderTree`), not sent as a separate `folders` field of `LibraryView`: one implementation serves the Tauri and the mock adapter, and the payload stays smaller.
- **Progressive scan = progress events per system folder** (`library-scan-progress`: folder, done/total, ROMs so far). Entries arrive in one `LibraryView` when the scan ends (the real library takes ~1.7 s cold, M1-07). If Task 13 measures more than 3 s for the real library, add a tracker row to stream entries per system.
- **Art goes over IPC as raw bytes** (`read_art` → `tauri::ipc::Response` → blob URL), not as a scoped asset-protocol URL: the asset protocol stays disabled, so the webview cannot name a path at all. CSP `img-src` becomes `'self' data: blob:`.
- **Choosing the ROMS folder itself works**: while planning, `coverly audit /Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/ROMS` reported **0 ROMs** (`ROMs folder 'ROMS' not found`) because the Anbernic profile expects the SD root. `profiles::fit_to_root` (Task 1) scans the picked folder directly when the profile's art lives inside system folders.
- **Language switch** (Auto / English / Español) is stored in `localStorage` until M5 settings exist; it is needed to E2E both languages.
- **Real data has 7,473 ROMs (< 10k)**: the "≥ 10k" exit criterion is demonstrated on a generated 12,000-ROM tree through the real Rust command path (Task 3) and a 12,000-entry fixture in the UI (Tasks 7, 10, 13); the real library is opened and timed read-only as well (Task 13).
- **Profile confirmation is read-only in M3** (spec §7.1 "user confirms"): the detected profile is shown in the toolbar and `open_library` already accepts `profileId`, but a picker to override it is deferred to M5 (profiles editor). A folder with no recognisable layout (e.g. a ROMs folder with no art folders at all) gets the translated `no-profile` message. New tracker row M3-08 in Task 13.
- **Deferred to later milestones** (new tracker rows in Task 13): "detected removable volumes" on the Start screen (spec §8) → M5; filter by identification method/confidence (spec §8 Filters) → M4, because identity is on demand in M3; drawer "candidates" and the change/search/from-file/remove actions → M4 (shown disabled).

`crates/coverly-core/tests/scan_progress.rs`:

```rust
mod common;

use common::{profile, write};
use coverly_core::scan::{scan_library, scan_library_with_progress, ScanOptions, ScanProgress};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use tempfile::TempDir;

#[test]
fn reports_one_event_per_system_folder() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let root = dir.path();
    write(root, "ROMS/SFC/A.sfc", "a");
    write(root, "ROMS/SFC/B.sfc", "b");
    write(root, "ROMS/GBA/C.gba", "c");
    write(root, "ROMS/APPS/tool.sh", "t");
    write(root, "ROMS/.hidden/x.sfc", "x");
    let db = SystemDb::builtin();
    let p = profile("anbernic-stock");
    let opts = ScanOptions::default();

    let mut events = Vec::new();
    let scan = scan_library_with_progress(root, &p, &db, &opts, &mut |e| events.push(e));

    assert_eq!(events.len(), 4, "{events:?}");
    assert_eq!(
        events[0],
        ScanProgress {
            done: 0,
            total: 3,
            folder: String::new(),
            roms: 0
        }
    );
    let last = events.last().unwrap();
    assert_eq!((last.done, last.total, last.roms), (3, 3, 3));
    let mut folders: Vec<String> = events[1..].iter().map(|e| e.folder.clone()).collect();
    folders.sort();
    assert_eq!(folders, ["APPS", "GBA", "SFC"]);
    assert!(events.windows(2).all(|w| w[0].done + 1 == w[1].done));
    assert_eq!(scan, scan_library(root, &p, &db, &opts));
}

#[test]
fn a_missing_roms_folder_sends_no_progress() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let mut events = Vec::new();
    let scan = scan_library_with_progress(
        dir.path(),
        &profile("anbernic-stock"),
        &SystemDb::builtin(),
        &ScanOptions::default(),
        &mut |e| events.push(e),
    );
    assert!(events.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(scan.warnings.len(), 1);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `scan_library_with_progress`**

In `crates/coverly-core/src/scan/mod.rs`, add the struct after `ScanWarning`:

```rust
/// Progress of [`scan_library_with_progress`]: one event before the first
/// folder (`done == 0`, empty `folder`) and one after each folder of the
/// ROMs root, known or not. No event is sent when the ROMs folder is
/// missing or unreadable (the scan then only holds a warning).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ScanProgress {
    /// Folders of the ROMs root scanned so far.
    pub done: usize,
    /// Folders of the ROMs root to scan (hidden and skipped ones excluded).
    pub total: usize,
    /// Name of the folder just scanned (empty in the first event).
    pub folder: String,
    /// ROMs found so far.
    pub roms: usize,
}
```

Replace the body of `scan_library` with a delegation and move the existing body into the new function, collecting the folders first so `total` is known:

```rust
pub fn scan_library(
    root: &Path,
    profile: &Profile,
    systems: &SystemDb,
    opts: &ScanOptions,
) -> LibraryScan {
    scan_library_with_progress(root, profile, systems, opts, &mut |_| {})
}

/// [`scan_library`], calling `progress` before the first system folder and
/// after each one (see [`ScanProgress`]).
pub fn scan_library_with_progress(
    root: &Path,
    profile: &Profile,
    systems: &SystemDb,
    opts: &ScanOptions,
    progress: &mut dyn FnMut(ScanProgress),
) -> LibraryScan {
    let mut scan = LibraryScan {
        root: root.to_path_buf(),
        profile_id: profile.id.clone(),
        systems: Vec::new(),
        warnings: Vec::new(),
    };
    let Some(roms_dir) = find_dir_ignoring_case(root, &profile.roms_root) else {
        scan.warnings.push(ScanWarning {
            path: root.join(&profile.roms_root),
            message: format!("ROMs folder `{}` not found", profile.roms_root),
        });
        return scan;
    };
    let entries = match fs::read_dir(&roms_dir) {
        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(err) => {
            scan.warnings.push(io_warning(&roms_dir, &err));
            return scan;
        }
    };

    let mut dirs: Vec<(String, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
    for entry in entries {
        let entry = match entry {
            Ok(entry) => entry,
            Err(err) => {
                scan.warnings.push(io_warning(&roms_dir, &err));
                continue;
            }
        };
        let folder_name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
        if is_skipped_entry(&folder_name, opts) {
            continue;
        }
        let dir = entry.path();
        let Ok(file_type) = entry.file_type() else {
            continue;
        };
        if !matches!(kind(&dir, file_type, opts, &mut scan.warnings), Kind::Dir) {
            continue;
        }
        dirs.push((folder_name, dir));
    }

    let total = dirs.len();
    let mut found = 0;
    progress(ScanProgress {
        done: 0,
        total,
        folder: String::new(),
        roms: 0,
    });
    for (index, (folder_name, dir)) in dirs.into_iter().enumerate() {
        let system = systems.resolve_folder(&folder_name);
        let roms = system
            .map(|s| scan_system(&dir, s, profile, opts, &mut scan.warnings))
            .unwrap_or_default();
        found += roms.len();
        progress(ScanProgress {
            done: index + 1,
            total,
            folder: folder_name.clone(),
            roms: found,
        });
        scan.systems.push(SystemScan {
            dir,
            folder_name,
            system_id: system.map(|s| s.id.clone()),
            roms,
        });
    }
    scan.systems.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.folder_name
            .to_lowercase()
            .cmp(&b.folder_name.to_lowercase())
            .then_with(|| a.folder_name.cmp(&b.folder_name))
    });
    scan
}
```

Also change `fn find_dir_ignoring_case` to `pub(crate) fn find_dir_ignoring_case` (Step 7 uses it).

```rust
mod common;

use common::{profile, write};
use coverly_core::art::{resolve_art, ArtState};
use coverly_core::profiles::{fit_to_root, Media};
use coverly_core::scan::{scan_library, ScanOptions};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use tempfile::TempDir;

#[test]
fn picking_the_roms_folder_itself_scans_it_directly() {
    // The user picked `…/ROMS`, not the SD root.
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "SFC/Zelda (USA).sfc", "z");
    write(dir.path(), "SFC/Imgs/Zelda (USA).png", "png");
    write(dir.path(), "APPS/tool.sh", "t");
    let db = SystemDb::builtin();

    let fitted = fit_to_root(&profile("anbernic-stock"), dir.path(), &db);
    assert_eq!(fitted.roms_root, "");

    let scan = scan_library(dir.path(), &fitted, &db, &ScanOptions::default());
    assert!(scan.warnings.is_empty(), "{:?}", scan.warnings);
    let sfc = scan.systems.iter().find(|s| s.folder_name == "SFC").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sfc.roms.len(), 1);
    let art = resolve_art(&scan, &fitted, &db, &[Media::Cover]);
    let sfc_art = art.iter().find(|s| s.scan.folder_name == "SFC").unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(
        sfc_art.roms[0].art[&Media::Cover],
        ArtState::Present { .. }
    ));
}

#[test]

    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "ROMS/SFC/Zelda (USA).sfc", "z");

    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "Photos/a.jpg", "j");

#[test]

    assert_eq!(fitted, profile("esde"));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement `fit_to_root`**

`crates/coverly-core/src/profiles/fit.rs`:

```rust
//! Fitting a profile to the folder the user actually picked.

use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

use super::Profile;
use crate::scan::find_dir_ignoring_case;
use crate::systems::SystemDb;

/// The profile to scan `root` with.
///
/// Users often pick the ROMs folder itself (`…/ROMS`) instead of the SD
/// root. When `root` has no `roms_root` folder, every slot of the profile
/// lives inside the system folder (templates start with `{system_dir}`)
/// and a child of `root` is a known system folder, the returned profile
/// scans `root` directly (`roms_root = ""`). Otherwise `profile` is
/// returned unchanged. Read-only.
pub fn fit_to_root(profile: &Profile, root: &Path, systems: &SystemDb) -> Profile {
    let mut fitted = profile.clone();
    if profile.roms_root.is_empty() || find_dir_ignoring_case(root, &profile.roms_root).is_some()
    {
        return fitted;
    }
    let art_inside_system_dirs = profile
        .slots
        .values()
        .all(|slot| slot.template.starts_with("{system_dir}"));
    if !art_inside_system_dirs {
        return fitted;
    }
    let has_system_folder = fs::read_dir(root)
        .map(|entries| {
            entries.flatten().any(|e| {
                e.path().is_dir()
                    && systems
                        .resolve_folder(&e.file_name().to_string_lossy())
                        .is_some()
            })
        })
        .unwrap_or(false);
    if has_system_folder {
        fitted.roms_root = String::new();
    }
    fitted
}
```

```rust
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;

use coverly_core::scan::ScanOptions;
use coverly_core::settings::{LibrarySettings, RecentLibrary, LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE, MAX_RECENT};
use tempfile::TempDir;

fn recent(path: &str, opened_at: i64) -> RecentLibrary {
    RecentLibrary {
        path: PathBuf::from(path),
        name: path.rsplit('/').next().unwrap().to_string(),
        profile_id: "anbernic-stock".into(),
        roms: 10,
        opened_at,
    }
}

#[test]
fn missing_file_gives_defaults() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let s = LibrarySettings::load(&dir.path().join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE));
    assert_eq!(s, LibrarySettings::default());
    assert_eq!(s.version, 1);
    assert_eq!(s.ignored_dirs, ScanOptions::default().ignored_dirs);
    assert!(s.recent.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn save_and_load_round_trip_creating_folders() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("nested/config").join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE);
    let mut s = LibrarySettings::default();
    s.remember(recent("/Volumes/SD", 5));
    s.save(&path).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(LibrarySettings::load(&path), s);
    assert!(!path.with_extension("json.tmp").exists(), "temp file left behind");
}

#[test]
fn corrupt_file_falls_back_to_defaults_and_is_kept_aside() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE);
    fs::write(&path, "{ not json").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(LibrarySettings::load(&path), LibrarySettings::default());

    let path = dir.path().join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE);
    fs::write(&path, r#"{"future_field": true, "ignored_dirs": ["bios"]}"#).unwrap();
    let s = LibrarySettings::load(&path);
    assert_eq!(s.ignored_dirs, ["bios"]);

}

    for i in 0..12 {
        s.remember(recent(&format!("/lib/{i}"), i));
    }

    );
}

    s.remember(recent("/a", 1));
    s.remember(recent("/b", 2));

    assert_eq!(s.recent[0].path, PathBuf::from("/b"));

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the module**

`crates/coverly-core/src/settings/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! Persisted settings. M3 needs the "Library" group of spec §9: ignored
//! folders and the recent libraries list, stored as JSON in the app config
//! dir (see [`crate::paths::config_dir`]). Loading never fails: a missing
//! file gives the defaults and a corrupt one is renamed to
//! `library.json.bad` and replaced by the defaults.

use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::scan::ScanOptions;

/// File name of the library settings inside the config dir.
pub const LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE: &str = "library.json";
/// Recent libraries kept.
pub const MAX_RECENT: usize = 10;

/// One library the user opened.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RecentLibrary {
    /// Folder as the user picked it.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// Display name (the folder name).
    pub name: String,
    /// Profile it was opened with.
    pub profile_id: String,
    /// ROMs found the last time.
    pub roms: u32,
    /// When it was last opened, in Unix seconds.
    pub opened_at: i64,
}

/// The "Library" settings group.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct LibrarySettings {
    /// Format version of the file.
    pub version: u32,
    /// Folder names never scanned (case-insensitive, at any depth).
    pub ignored_dirs: Vec<String>,
    /// Most recent first, at most [`MAX_RECENT`].
    pub recent: Vec<RecentLibrary>,
}

impl Default for LibrarySettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        LibrarySettings {
            version: 1,
            ignored_dirs: ScanOptions::default().ignored_dirs,
            recent: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

impl LibrarySettings {
    /// Reads `path`; defaults when it is missing or unreadable. A file that
    /// is not valid JSON is renamed to `<name>.bad` first, so it is never
    /// silently overwritten.
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> LibrarySettings {
        let Ok(text) = fs::read_to_string(path) else {
            return LibrarySettings::default();
        };
        match serde_json::from_str::<LibrarySettings>(&text) {
            Ok(mut settings) => {
                let dirs = std::mem::take(&mut settings.ignored_dirs);
                settings.set_ignored_dirs(dirs);
                settings.recent.truncate(MAX_RECENT);
                settings
            }
            Err(_) => {
                let _ = fs::rename(path, path.with_extension("json.bad"));
                LibrarySettings::default()
            }
        }
    }

    /// Writes `path` atomically (temp file + rename), creating its folder.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        let text = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self).map_err(io::Error::other)?;
        let tmp = path.with_extension("json.tmp");
        fs::write(&tmp, text)?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path)
    }

    /// Puts `entry` first, dropping an older entry with the same path and
    /// anything beyond [`MAX_RECENT`].
    pub fn remember(&mut self, entry: RecentLibrary) {
        self.recent.retain(|r| r.path != entry.path);
        self.recent.insert(0, entry);
        self.recent.truncate(MAX_RECENT);
    }

    /// Drops the library at `path` from the recent list.
    pub fn forget(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        self.recent.retain(|r| r.path != path);
    }

    /// Sets the ignored folders: trimmed, without empty names, `.`/`..` or
    /// names holding a path separator, without case-insensitive duplicates
    /// (the first spelling wins).
    pub fn set_ignored_dirs(&mut self, dirs: Vec<String>) {
        let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for dir in dirs {
            let dir = dir.trim();
            if dir.is_empty() || dir == "." || dir == ".." || dir.contains(['/', '\\']) {
                continue;
            }
            if out.iter().any(|o| o.to_lowercase() == dir.to_lowercase()) {
                continue;
            }
            out.push(dir.to_string());
        }
        self.ignored_dirs = out;
    }

    /// Scan options with these ignored folders and the other defaults.
    pub fn scan_options(&self) -> ScanOptions {
        ScanOptions {
            ignored_dirs: self.ignored_dirs.clone(),
            ..ScanOptions::default()
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Fixture libraries for the app's integration tests.

#![allow(dead_code)] // each test binary uses a different subset

use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

use coverly_core::scan::ScanOptions;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::view::LibraryView;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::{open_session, OpenRequest, Session};
use tempfile::TempDir;

/// Twenty folder names the built-in system database resolves.
pub const TWENTY_SYSTEMS: [&str; 20] = [
    "A2600", "A7800", "A800", "ATARIST", "C64", "FC", "FDS", "GB", "GBA", "GBC", "GG", "LYNX",
    "MD", "MSX", "NDS", "NGP", "PCE", "SFC", "SMS", "WS",
];

/// Writes `bytes` to `root/rel`, creating parent folders.
pub fn write(root: &Path, rel: &str, bytes: &[u8]) {
    let path = root.join(rel);
    fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    fs::write(path, bytes).unwrap();
}

/// An Anbernic-stock SD (`anbernic/`, `ROMS/<SYS>/…`) with `per_system`
/// ROMs `Game NNNNN (USA).<first extension of the system>` in each folder;
/// every `art_every`-th ROM (0 = none) has a cover in `Imgs`.
pub fn anbernic_tree(folders: &[&str], per_system: usize, art_every: usize) -> TempDir {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let db = SystemDb::builtin();
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.path().join("anbernic")).unwrap();
    for folder in folders {
        let system = db
            .resolve_folder(folder)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{folder} is not a known system folder"));
        let ext = system
            .extensions
            .first()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{folder} lists no extensions"));
        for i in 0..per_system {
            let stem = format!("Game {i:05} (USA)");
            write(dir.path(), &format!("ROMS/{folder}/{stem}.{ext}"), b"x");
            if art_every > 0 && i % art_every == 0 {
                write(dir.path(), &format!("ROMS/{folder}/Imgs/{stem}.png"), b"png");
            }
        }
    }
    dir
}

/// Opens `path` with default scan options as generation 7.
pub fn open(path: &Path, profile_id: Option<&str>) -> Result<(Session, LibraryView), IpcError> {
    open_session(
        &OpenRequest {
            path,
            profile_id,
            scan: &ScanOptions::default(),
            generation: 7,
        },
        &SystemDb::builtin(),
        &mut |_| {},
    )
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use common::{anbernic_tree, open, write};
use coverly_core::identify::names::Region;
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::scan::{ScanOptions, ScanProgress};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::view::ArtMark;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::{open_session, OpenRequest};
use serde_json::json;
use tempfile::TempDir;

#[test]
fn opens_an_anbernic_sd_with_art_status() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["SFC", "GBA"], 4, 2);
    let (session, view) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();

    assert_eq!(view.generation, 7);
    assert_eq!(view.profile.id, "anbernic-stock");
    assert!(view.profile.detected);
    assert!(!view.profile.fitted);
    assert_eq!(view.profile.media, vec![Media::Cover]);
    assert_eq!(view.entries.len(), 8);
    assert_eq!(session.entries.len(), 8);
    assert_eq!(session.generation, 7);
    assert!(view
        .entries
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .all(|(i, e)| e.id as usize == i));
    let present = view
        .entries
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| e.art.cover == ArtMark::Present)
        .count();
    assert_eq!(present, 4);
    assert!(view
        .entries
        .iter()
        .all(|e| e.art.screenshot == ArtMark::Unsupported && e.art.title == ArtMark::Unsupported));

    // Folders are sorted by name: GBA before SFC.
    let first = &view.entries[0];
    let system = &view.systems[first.system as usize];
    assert_eq!(system.folder, "GBA");
    assert_eq!(system.system_id.as_deref(), Some("gba"));
    assert_eq!(system.name, "Game Boy Advance");
    assert_eq!(system.dir, "ROMS/GBA");
    assert_eq!(system.roms, 4);
    assert_eq!(first.rel_path, "ROMS/GBA/Game 00000 (USA).gba");
    assert_eq!(first.dir, "ROMS/GBA");
    assert_eq!(first.name, "Game 00000 (USA)");
    assert_eq!(first.ext, "gba");
    assert_eq!(first.size, 1);
    assert!(first.mtime > 1_600_000_000);
    assert_eq!(first.regions, vec![Region::Usa]);
    assert!(session.entries[0].art.contains_key(&Media::Cover));
    assert_eq!(view.name, tree.path().file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy());
}

#[test]
fn opens_the_roms_folder_itself() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["SFC", "GBA"], 4, 2);
    let (_, view) = open(&tree.path().join("ROMS"), None).unwrap();
    assert!(view.profile.fitted);
    assert_eq!(view.entries.len(), 8);
    assert_eq!(view.entries[0].rel_path, "GBA/Game 00000 (USA).gba");
    assert_eq!(view.entries[0].dir, "GBA");
}

#[test]
fn an_explicit_profile_skips_detection() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let (_, view) = open(tree.path(), Some("garlicos")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.profile.id, "garlicos");
    assert!(!view.profile.detected);
}

#[test]
fn missing_folder_is_not_found() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let missing = dir.path().join("nope");
    assert_eq!(
        open(&missing, None).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::NotFound(missing.display().to_string())
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_file_is_not_a_folder() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "file.txt", b"x");
    let file = dir.path().join("file.txt");
    assert_eq!(
        open(&file, None).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::NotAFolder(file.display().to_string())
    );
}

#[test]
fn folder_without_layout_is_no_profile() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(
        open(dir.path(), None).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::NoProfile(_)
    ));
}

#[test]
fn unknown_profile_is_rejected() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    assert_eq!(
        open(tree.path(), Some("nope")).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::UnknownProfile("nope".into())
    );
}

#[test]
fn progress_reaches_the_total() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["SFC", "GBA", "MD"], 2, 0);
    let mut events: Vec<ScanProgress> = Vec::new();
    open_session(
        &OpenRequest {
            path: tree.path(),
            profile_id: None,
            scan: &ScanOptions::default(),
            generation: 1,
        },
        &SystemDb::builtin(),
        &mut |p| events.push(p),
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(events.first().unwrap().done, 0);
    let last = events.last().unwrap();
    assert_eq!((last.done, last.total, last.roms), (3, 3, 6));
}

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn hostile_names_are_kept_verbatim() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 0, 0);
    write(tree.path(), "ROMS/GBA/<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>.gba", b"x");
    write(tree.path(), "ROMS/GBA/\u{202E}gnp.gba", b"x");
    let (_, view) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    let names: Vec<&str> = view.entries.iter().map(|e| e.name.as_str()).collect();
    assert!(names.contains(&"<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>"), "{names:?}");
    assert!(names.contains(&"\u{202E}gnp"), "{names:?}");
}

#[test]
fn wire_format_is_snake_case_with_kebab_errors() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 1);
    let (_, view) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    let json = serde_json::to_value(&view).unwrap();
    let entry = &json["entries"][0];
    for key in [
        "id", "system", "rel_path", "dir", "name", "ext", "size", "mtime", "regions", "art",
    ] {
        assert!(entry.get(key).is_some(), "entry lacks {key}: {entry}");
    }
    assert_eq!(entry["art"]["cover"], "present");
    assert_eq!(entry["art"]["title"], "unsupported");
    assert_eq!(entry["regions"][0], "usa");
    assert_eq!(json["profile"]["media"], json!(["cover"]));
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::to_value(IpcError::NotFound("/x".into())).unwrap(),
        json!({ "code": "not-found", "detail": "/x" })
    );
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::to_value(IpcError::NoLibrary).unwrap(),
        json!({ "code": "no-library" })
    );
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::to_value(IpcError::UnknownEntry(5)).unwrap(),
        json!({ "code": "unknown-entry", "detail": 5 })
    );
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use common::{anbernic_tree, open, TWENTY_SYSTEMS};

#[test]
fn opens_twelve_thousand_roms() {
    // 20 systems × 600 ROMs, every other one with a cover.
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&TWENTY_SYSTEMS, 600, 2);
    let started = Instant::now();
    let (_, view) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    let elapsed = started.elapsed();
    let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&view).unwrap().len();
    eprintln!("12k tree: open {elapsed:?}, LibraryView JSON {bytes} bytes");
    assert_eq!(view.entries.len(), 12_000);
    assert_eq!(view.systems.iter().filter(|s| s.roms == 600).count(), 20);
    assert!(elapsed < Duration::from_secs(20), "open took {elapsed:?}");
    assert!(bytes < 6 * 1024 * 1024, "LibraryView is {bytes} bytes");
}
```

```rust
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Instant;

use coverly_core::scan::ScanOptions;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::view::ArtMark;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::{open_session, OpenRequest};

/// Read-only: scans and resolves art like the `open_library` command.
#[test]
#[ignore = "reads a real ROM library: COVERLY_REAL_ROMS=<dir> cargo test -p coverly-roms --release --test real_library -- --ignored --nocapture"]
fn opens_a_real_library_read_only() {
    let Some(root) = std::env::var_os("COVERLY_REAL_ROMS") else {
        eprintln!("COVERLY_REAL_ROMS is not set: nothing to do");
        return;
    };
    let root = PathBuf::from(root);
    let mut events = 0usize;
    let started = Instant::now();
    let (_, view) = open_session(
        &OpenRequest {
            path: &root,
            profile_id: None,
            scan: &ScanOptions::default(),
            generation: 1,
        },
        &SystemDb::builtin(),
        &mut |_| events += 1,
    )
    .expect("the real library opens");
    let elapsed = started.elapsed();
    let json_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&view).unwrap().len();
    let missing_cover = view
        .entries
        .iter()
        .filter(|e| e.art.cover == ArtMark::Missing)
        .count();
    let systems = view.systems.iter().filter(|s| s.roms > 0).count();
    eprintln!(
        "root={} profile={} fitted={} systems_with_roms={systems} folders={} entries={} \
         missing_cover={missing_cover} warnings={} progress_events={events} open={elapsed:?} \
         scan_ms={} json_bytes={json_bytes}",
        root.display(),
        view.profile.id,
        view.profile.fitted,
        view.systems.len(),
        view.entries.len(),
        view.warnings.len(),
        view.scan_ms,
    );
    assert!(!view.entries.is_empty());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `IpcError`**

`src-tauri/src/error.rs`:

```rust
//! Errors returned by IPC commands. They cross the bridge as
//! `{ "code": "<kebab-case>", "detail": … }` (no `detail` for unit
//! variants); the UI shows the i18n key `errors.<code>`. Codes are stable:
//! never rename one.

use serde::Serialize;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, thiserror::Error)]
#[serde(tag = "code", content = "detail", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum IpcError {
    #[error("folder not found: {0}")]
    NotFound(String),
    #[error("not a folder: {0}")]
    NotAFolder(String),
    #[error("cannot read {0}")]
    Unreadable(String),
    #[error("no layout profile detected in {0}")]
    NoProfile(String),
    #[error("unknown layout profile `{0}`")]
    UnknownProfile(String),
    #[error("no library is open")]
    NoLibrary,
    #[error("the library was closed or reopened")]
    StaleLibrary,
    #[error("another library was opened meanwhile")]
    Superseded,
    #[error("unknown entry {0}")]
    UnknownEntry(u32),
    #[error("no {0} image for this entry")]
    ArtMissing(String),
    #[error("art outside the library: {0}")]
    OutsideLibrary(String),
    #[error("image too large: {0} bytes")]
    ArtTooLarge(u64),
    #[error("{0}")]
    Io(String),
    #[error("cannot save settings: {0}")]
    Settings(String),
}
```

`src-tauri/src/library/view.rs`:

```rust
//! What the webview sees of a library: compact, serialisable, path-free
//! except for display strings. Field names are the wire contract mirrored
//! by `src/ipc/types.ts` (pinned by `tests/contract.rs`).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use coverly_core::art::ArtState;
use coverly_core::identify::names::Region;
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::scan::ScanProgress;
use serde::Serialize;

/// State of one media for one entry.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum ArtMark {
    Present,
    Missing,
    Unsupported,
}

/// State of every media for one entry.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ArtMarks {
    pub cover: ArtMark,
    pub screenshot: ArtMark,
    pub title: ArtMark,
}

impl ArtMarks {
    /// Marks from the core's art states (absent media are unsupported).
    pub fn from_states(states: &BTreeMap<Media, ArtState>) -> ArtMarks {
        let mark = |media: Media| match states.get(&media) {
            Some(ArtState::Present { .. }) => ArtMark::Present,
            Some(ArtState::Missing) => ArtMark::Missing,
            _ => ArtMark::Unsupported,
        };
        ArtMarks {
            cover: mark(Media::Cover),
            screenshot: mark(Media::Screenshot),
            title: mark(Media::Title),
        }
    }
}

/// One ROM.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct EntryView {
    /// Index in [`LibraryView::entries`]; the handle for every later call.
    pub id: u32,
    /// Index in [`LibraryView::systems`].
    pub system: u16,
    /// Primary file relative to the library root, `/`-separated.
    pub rel_path: String,
    /// Folder of `rel_path` (`""` at the root).
    pub dir: String,
    /// File name without extension.
    pub name: String,
    /// Extension, lowercase, without the dot.
    pub ext: String,
    /// Bytes, companions included.
    pub size: u64,
    /// Last modification, Unix seconds.
    pub mtime: i64,
    /// Regions parsed from the name.
    pub regions: Vec<Region>,
    pub art: ArtMarks,
}

/// One folder of the ROMs root.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct SystemView {
    pub index: u16,
    /// Folder name as on disk, e.g. `SFC`.
    pub folder: String,
    /// Folder relative to the library root, `/`-separated.
    pub dir: String,
    /// `None` for folders that match no system (listed, not scanned).
    pub system_id: Option<String>,
    /// System display name, or the folder name when unknown.
    pub name: String,
    pub roms: u32,
}

/// The layout profile used.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ProfileView {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    /// Media this layout can store, in display order.
    pub media: Vec<Media>,
    /// Auto-detected (vs chosen by id).
    pub detected: bool,
    /// Markers that were found when detected.
    pub evidence: Vec<String>,
    /// The picked folder is the ROMs folder itself (see `fit_to_root`).
    pub fitted: bool,
}

/// A problem met while scanning.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct WarningView {
    pub path: String,
    pub message: String,
}

/// Everything the explorer shows.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct LibraryView {
    /// Identifies this opening; later calls must pass it back.
    pub generation: u64,
    /// The folder as picked, for display.
    pub root: String,
    /// Its last component.
    pub name: String,
    pub profile: ProfileView,
    pub systems: Vec<SystemView>,
    pub entries: Vec<EntryView>,
    pub warnings: Vec<WarningView>,
    /// Scan + art resolution time.
    pub scan_ms: u64,
}

/// Payload of the `library-scan-progress` event.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ScanProgressEvent {
    pub generation: u64,
    pub done: usize,
    pub total: usize,
    pub folder: String,
    pub roms: usize,
}

impl ScanProgressEvent {
    pub fn new(generation: u64, p: ScanProgress) -> ScanProgressEvent {
        ScanProgressEvent {
            generation,
            done: p.done,
            total: p.total,
            folder: p.folder,
            roms: p.roms,
        }
    }
}

/// A recent library, with whether its folder is reachable right now.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct RecentView {
    pub path: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub profile_id: String,
    pub roms: u32,
    pub opened_at: i64,
    pub available: bool,
}

/// The library settings the explorer edits.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct LibrarySettingsView {
    pub ignored_dirs: Vec<String>,
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `open_session`**

`src-tauri/src/library/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! An opened library: scanning a folder into a [`LibraryView`] for the UI,
//! and keeping, per entry, the paths only the backend may touch.

pub mod view;

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::{Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use coverly_core::art::{resolve_art, ArtState};
use coverly_core::identify::names::parse_name;
use coverly_core::profiles::{builtin_profiles, detect, fit_to_root, Media, Profile};
use coverly_core::scan::{scan_library_with_progress, RomEntry, ScanOptions, ScanProgress};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;

use crate::error::IpcError;
use view::{ArtMarks, EntryView, LibraryView, ProfileView, SystemView, WarningView};

/// Backend-only data of one entry (same index as its [`EntryView`]).
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct SessionEntry {
    pub system_id: String,
    pub rom: RomEntry,
    /// Art files that exist, per media.
    pub art: BTreeMap<Media, PathBuf>,
}

/// The library currently open in the app.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Session {
    pub generation: u64,
    /// The folder as picked.
    pub root: PathBuf,
    /// `root` canonicalised: nothing outside it is ever read.
    pub root_canon: PathBuf,
    pub entries: Vec<SessionEntry>,
}

/// What to open.
pub struct OpenRequest<'a> {
    pub path: &'a Path,
    /// A built-in profile id, or `None` to auto-detect.
    pub profile_id: Option<&'a str>,
    pub scan: &'a ScanOptions,
    pub generation: u64,
}

/// Checks the folder, picks (or detects) the profile, fits it to the
/// folder, scans with `progress` and resolves every media's art state.
/// Read-only.
pub fn open_session(
    req: &OpenRequest<'_>,
    systems: &SystemDb,
    progress: &mut dyn FnMut(ScanProgress),
) -> Result<(Session, LibraryView), IpcError> {
    let root = req.path;
    let shown = root.display().to_string();
    match fs::metadata(root) {
        Ok(meta) if meta.is_dir() => {}
        Ok(_) => return Err(IpcError::NotAFolder(shown)),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Err(IpcError::NotFound(shown)),
        Err(_) => return Err(IpcError::Unreadable(shown)),
    }
    let root_canon = fs::canonicalize(root).map_err(|_| IpcError::Unreadable(shown.clone()))?;
    let (base, detected, evidence) = choose_profile(root, req.profile_id)?;
    let profile = fit_to_root(&base, root, systems);
    let fitted = profile.roms_root != base.roms_root;

    let started = Instant::now();
    let scan = scan_library_with_progress(root, &profile, systems, req.scan, progress);
    let art = resolve_art(&scan, &profile, systems, &Media::ALL);
    let scan_ms = u64::try_from(started.elapsed().as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);

    let mut warnings: Vec<WarningView> = scan.warnings.iter().map(warning).collect();
    let mut system_views = Vec::with_capacity(art.len());
    let mut entry_views = Vec::new();
    let mut entries = Vec::new();
    for system_art in &art {
        let index = system_views.len() as u16;
        let known = system_art
            .scan
            .system_id
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|id| systems.get(id));
        system_views.push(SystemView {
            index,
            folder: system_art.scan.folder_name.clone(),
            dir: rel_string(root, &system_art.scan.dir),
            system_id: system_art.scan.system_id.clone(),
            name: known
                .map(|s| s.name.clone())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| system_art.scan.folder_name.clone()),
            roms: system_art.roms.len() as u32,
        });
        warnings.extend(system_art.warnings.iter().map(warning));
        for rom_art in &system_art.roms {
            let rom = &rom_art.rom;
            let rel_path = rel_string(root, &rom.path);
            let dir = rel_path
                .rsplit_once('/')
                .map(|(d, _)| d.to_string())
                .unwrap_or_default();
            entry_views.push(EntryView {
                id: entries.len() as u32,
                system: index,
                rel_path,
                dir,
                name: rom.stem.clone(),
                ext: rom.ext.clone(),
                size: rom.size,
                mtime: unix_secs(rom.modified),
                regions: parse_name(&rom.stem, false).regions,
                art: ArtMarks::from_states(&rom_art.art),
            });
            entries.push(SessionEntry {
                system_id: system_art.scan.system_id.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                rom: rom.clone(),
                art: rom_art
                    .art
                    .iter()
                    .filter_map(|(media, state)| match state {
                        ArtState::Present { path } => Some((*media, path.clone())),
                        _ => None,
                    })
                    .collect(),
            });
        }
    }

    let view = LibraryView {
        generation: req.generation,
        root: shown.clone(),
        name: root
            .file_name()
            .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
            .unwrap_or(shown),
        profile: ProfileView {
            id: profile.id.clone(),
            name: profile.name.clone(),
            media: Media::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|m| profile.slot(*m).is_some())
                .collect(),
            detected,
            evidence,
            fitted,
        },
        systems: system_views,
        entries: entry_views,
        warnings,
        scan_ms,
    };
    let session = Session {
        generation: req.generation,
        root: root.to_path_buf(),
        root_canon,
        entries,
    };
    Ok((session, view))
}

/// The requested built-in profile, or the best detected one.
fn choose_profile(root: &Path, id: Option<&str>) -> Result<(Profile, bool, Vec<String>), IpcError> {
    let all = builtin_profiles();
    if let Some(id) = id {
        return all
            .into_iter()
            .find(|p| p.id.eq_ignore_ascii_case(id))
            .map(|p| (p, false, Vec::new()))
            .ok_or_else(|| IpcError::UnknownProfile(id.to_string()));
    }
    let found = detect(root);
    let best = found
        .first()
        .ok_or_else(|| IpcError::NoProfile(root.display().to_string()))?;
    let profile = all
        .into_iter()
        .find(|p| p.id == best.profile_id)
        .expect("detect only returns built-in profile ids");
    Ok((profile, true, best.evidence.clone()))
}

/// `path` relative to `root`, `/`-separated (lossy for non-UTF-8 names).
fn rel_string(root: &Path, path: &Path) -> String {
    path.strip_prefix(root)
        .unwrap_or(path)
        .components()
        .map(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy())
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("/")
}

fn unix_secs(t: SystemTime) -> i64 {
    t.duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| i64::try_from(d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(i64::MAX))
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

fn warning(w: &coverly_core::scan::ScanWarning) -> WarningView {
    WarningView {
        path: w.path.display().to_string(),
        message: w.message.clone(),
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/tests/art_access.rs`:

```rust
mod common;

use std::fs;

use common::{anbernic_tree, open, write};
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::art::{entry_path, read_art_bytes, MAX_ART_BYTES};
use tempfile::TempDir;

const PNG: &[u8] = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\nfake";

#[test]
fn reads_present_art_bytes() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    write(tree.path(), "ROMS/GBA/Imgs/Game 00000 (USA).png", PNG);
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Cover).unwrap(), PNG);
}

#[test]
fn missing_or_unsupported_media_is_art_missing() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Cover).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::ArtMissing("cover".into())
    );
    assert_eq!(
        read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Screenshot).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::ArtMissing("screenshot".into())
    );
}

#[test]
fn unknown_entry_is_rejected() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 1);
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        read_art_bytes(&session, 999, Media::Cover).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::UnknownEntry(999)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        entry_path(&session, 999).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::UnknownEntry(999)
    );
}

#[test]
fn entry_path_is_the_rom_inside_the_root() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    let path = entry_path(&session, 0).unwrap();
    assert!(path.ends_with("ROMS/GBA/Game 00000 (USA).gba"), "{path:?}");
}

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn symlinked_art_outside_the_library_is_refused() {
    let outside = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(outside.path(), "secret.png", PNG);
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    fs::create_dir_all(tree.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs")).unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(
        outside.path().join("secret.png"),
        tree.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/Game 00000 (USA).png"),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let (session, view) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        view.entries[0].art.cover,
        coverly_roms_lib::library::view::ArtMark::Present
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Cover).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::OutsideLibrary(_)
    ));
}

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn symlinked_art_folder_outside_is_refused() {
    let outside = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    write(outside.path(), "Game 00000 (USA).png", PNG);
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(outside.path(), tree.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs")).unwrap();
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    match read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Cover) {
        Err(IpcError::OutsideLibrary(_)) | Err(IpcError::ArtMissing(_)) => {}
        other => panic!("art outside the root was served: {other:?}"),
    }
}

#[test]
fn oversized_art_is_refused() {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 1);
    let art = tree.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/Game 00000 (USA).png");
    fs::File::options()
        .write(true)
        .open(&art)
        .unwrap()
        .set_len(MAX_ART_BYTES + 1)
        .unwrap();
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        read_art_bytes(&session, 0, Media::Cover).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::ArtTooLarge(MAX_ART_BYTES + 1)
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement scoped art reads**

`src-tauri/src/library/art.rs`:

```rust
//! Reading an entry's files for the webview, only inside the opened root.
//!
//! The webview never sends a path: it names an entry id and a media, and
//! the path comes from the scan. The path is canonicalised (resolving
//! symlinks) and must stay inside the canonical library root.

use std::fs::{self, File};
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use coverly_core::profiles::Media;

use super::{Session, SessionEntry};
use crate::error::IpcError;

/// Largest art file served (the core image pipeline's own input cap).
pub const MAX_ART_BYTES: u64 = 20 * 1024 * 1024;

fn media_name(media: Media) -> String {
    match media {
        Media::Cover => "cover",
        Media::Screenshot => "screenshot",
        Media::Title => "title",
    }
    .to_string()
}

fn entry(session: &Session, id: u32) -> Result<&SessionEntry, IpcError> {
    session
        .entries
        .get(id as usize)
        .ok_or(IpcError::UnknownEntry(id))
}

/// `path` canonicalised, if it stays inside the library.
fn inside_root(session: &Session, path: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, IpcError> {
    let canon =
        fs::canonicalize(path).map_err(|_| IpcError::Unreadable(path.display().to_string()))?;
    if canon.starts_with(&session.root_canon) {
        Ok(canon)
    } else {
        Err(IpcError::OutsideLibrary(path.display().to_string()))
    }
}

/// The bytes of `media` of entry `id`.
pub fn read_art_bytes(session: &Session, id: u32, media: Media) -> Result<Vec<u8>, IpcError> {
    let entry = entry(session, id)?;
    let path = entry
        .art
        .get(&media)
        .ok_or_else(|| IpcError::ArtMissing(media_name(media)))?;
    let canon = match inside_root(session, path) {
        Err(IpcError::Unreadable(_)) => return Err(IpcError::ArtMissing(media_name(media))),
        other => other?,
    };
    let meta = fs::metadata(&canon).map_err(|_| IpcError::ArtMissing(media_name(media)))?;
    if !meta.is_file() {
        return Err(IpcError::ArtMissing(media_name(media)));
    }
    if meta.len() > MAX_ART_BYTES {
        return Err(IpcError::ArtTooLarge(meta.len()));
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(meta.len() as usize);
    File::open(&canon)
        .and_then(|f| f.take(MAX_ART_BYTES + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes))
        .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
    if bytes.len() as u64 > MAX_ART_BYTES {
        return Err(IpcError::ArtTooLarge(bytes.len() as u64));
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

/// The primary file of entry `id`, checked to be inside the library.
pub fn entry_path(session: &Session, id: u32) -> Result<PathBuf, IpcError> {
    let entry = entry(session, id)?;
    inside_root(session, &entry.rom.path)
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use std::path::PathBuf;

use common::anbernic_tree;
use coverly_core::settings::{LibrarySettings, LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::view::ScanProgressEvent;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::{open_session, OpenRequest};
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;
use tempfile::TempDir;

fn state(config: &TempDir) -> AppState {
    AppState::new(
        config.path().join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE),
        config.path().join("cache"),
    )
}

#[test]
fn open_installs_the_session_and_remembers_the_library() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 3, 1);
    let st = state(&config);
    let mut events: Vec<ScanProgressEvent> = Vec::new();
    let view = st
        .open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |e| events.push(e))
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.generation, 1);
    assert!(events.iter().all(|e| e.generation == 1));
    assert_eq!(st.session(1).unwrap().entries.len(), 3);

    let recent = st.recent();
    assert_eq!(recent.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(recent[0].path, tree.path().display().to_string());
    assert_eq!(recent[0].roms, 3);
    assert!(recent[0].available);
    let saved = LibrarySettings::load(&config.path().join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE));
    assert_eq!(saved.recent.len(), 1);
}

#[test]
fn stale_generation_is_rejected() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let st = state(&config);
    assert_eq!(st.session(1).unwrap_err(), IpcError::NoLibrary);
    st.open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .unwrap();
    st.open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(st.session(1).unwrap_err(), IpcError::StaleLibrary);
    assert!(st.session(2).is_ok());
}

#[test]
fn a_newer_open_supersedes_an_older_one() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let st = state(&config);
    let older = st.begin_open();
    let newer = st.begin_open();
    let scan = |generation| {
        open_session(
            &OpenRequest {
                path: tree.path(),
                profile_id: None,
                scan: &Default::default(),
                generation,
            },
            &SystemDb::builtin(),
            &mut |_| {},
        )
        .unwrap()
        .0
    };
    st.install(scan(newer)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(st.install(scan(older)).unwrap_err(), IpcError::Superseded);
    assert_eq!(st.session(newer).unwrap().generation, newer);
}

#[test]
fn open_succeeds_when_settings_cannot_be_saved() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    // The settings "file" is a folder: saving fails, opening must not.
    let settings_path = config.path().join("as-dir");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&settings_path).unwrap();
    let st = AppState::new(settings_path, config.path().join("cache"));
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    assert!(st
        .open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .is_ok());
}

#[test]
fn failed_open_keeps_the_previous_library() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let st = state(&config);
    st.open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .unwrap();
    let missing = PathBuf::from("/definitely/not/here");
    assert!(st
        .open(&missing, None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .is_err());
    // The failed attempt took generation 2; the open library is still 1.
    assert!(st.session(1).is_ok());
}

#[test]
fn recents_report_unavailable_folders_and_can_be_forgotten() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 1, 0);
    let st = state(&config);
    st.open(tree.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {})
        .unwrap();
    let gone = tree.path().to_path_buf();
    drop(tree);
    let recent = st.recent();
    assert!(!recent[0].available);
    assert!(st.forget(&gone).unwrap().is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn ignored_dirs_are_saved_normalised() {
    let config = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&config);
    let view = st
        .set_ignored_dirs(vec![" bios ".into(), "a/b".into(), "Saves".into()])
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.ignored_dirs, ["bios", "Saves"]);
    assert_eq!(st.library_settings().ignored_dirs, ["bios", "Saves"]);
}
```

Note on `failed_open_keeps_the_previous_library`: a failed open must not clear the installed session, but `session(1)` would return `StaleLibrary` if `session()` compared against `latest`. `session()` therefore compares the requested generation with the **installed** session's generation, never with `latest`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `AppState`**

`src-tauri/src/state.rs`:

```rust
//! App-wide state behind the IPC commands: the open library session, the
//! generation counter that lets a newer open supersede an older one, and
//! the library settings file. Free of Tauri types so it is unit-testable.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, PoisonError};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use coverly_core::cache::{Cache, CACHE_FILE};
use coverly_core::settings::{LibrarySettings, RecentLibrary};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;

use crate::error::IpcError;
use crate::library::view::{LibrarySettingsView, LibraryView, RecentView, ScanProgressEvent};
use crate::library::{open_session, OpenRequest, Session};

pub struct AppState {
    latest: AtomicU64,
    session: Mutex<Option<Arc<Session>>>,
    settings_path: PathBuf,
    cache_dir: PathBuf,
}

impl AppState {
    pub fn new(settings_path: PathBuf, cache_dir: PathBuf) -> AppState {
        AppState {
            latest: AtomicU64::new(0),
            session: Mutex::new(None),
            settings_path,
            cache_dir,
        }
    }

    /// Reserves the generation of a new open; any older open in flight
    /// will be [`IpcError::Superseded`].
    pub fn begin_open(&self) -> u64 {
        self.latest.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1
    }

    /// Makes `session` the open library unless a newer open began.
    pub fn install(&self, session: Session) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
        let mut slot = self.session.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner);
        if self.latest.load(Ordering::SeqCst) != session.generation {
            return Err(IpcError::Superseded);
        }
        *slot = Some(Arc::new(session));
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The open library, if it is `generation`.
    pub fn session(&self, generation: u64) -> Result<Arc<Session>, IpcError> {
        let slot = self.session.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner);
        match slot.as_ref() {
            None => Err(IpcError::NoLibrary),
            Some(s) if s.generation != generation => Err(IpcError::StaleLibrary),
            Some(s) => Ok(Arc::clone(s)),
        }
    }

    /// Opens `path` (blocking), installs it and remembers it as recent. A
    /// settings file that cannot be written never fails the open.
    pub fn open(
        &self,
        path: &Path,
        profile_id: Option<&str>,
        systems: &SystemDb,
        emit: &mut dyn FnMut(ScanProgressEvent),
    ) -> Result<LibraryView, IpcError> {
        let mut settings = self.settings();
        let generation = self.begin_open();
        let (session, view) = open_session(
            &OpenRequest {
                path,
                profile_id,
                scan: &settings.scan_options(),
                generation,
            },
            systems,
            &mut |p| emit(ScanProgressEvent::new(generation, p)),
        )?;
        self.install(session)?;
        settings.remember(RecentLibrary {
            path: path.to_path_buf(),
            name: view.name.clone(),
            profile_id: view.profile.id.clone(),
            roms: u32::try_from(view.entries.len()).unwrap_or(u32::MAX),
            opened_at: now_unix(),
        });
        if let Err(e) = settings.save(&self.settings_path) {
            eprintln!(
                "warning: cannot save {}: {e}",
                self.settings_path.display()
            );
        }
        Ok(view)
    }

    pub fn settings(&self) -> LibrarySettings {
        LibrarySettings::load(&self.settings_path)
    }

    fn save(&self, settings: &LibrarySettings) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
        settings
            .save(&self.settings_path)
            .map_err(|e| IpcError::Settings(e.to_string()))
    }

    /// Recent libraries, most recent first, with their availability.
    pub fn recent(&self) -> Vec<RecentView> {
        recent_views(&self.settings())
    }

    pub fn forget(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<RecentView>, IpcError> {
        let mut settings = self.settings();
        settings.forget(path);
        self.save(&settings)?;
        Ok(recent_views(&settings))
    }

    pub fn library_settings(&self) -> LibrarySettingsView {
        LibrarySettingsView {
            ignored_dirs: self.settings().ignored_dirs,
        }
    }

    pub fn set_ignored_dirs(&self, dirs: Vec<String>) -> Result<LibrarySettingsView, IpcError> {
        let mut settings = self.settings();
        settings.set_ignored_dirs(dirs);
        self.save(&settings)?;
        Ok(LibrarySettingsView {
            ignored_dirs: settings.ignored_dirs,
        })
    }

    /// The shared SQLite cache (hash memo, DATs), created on first use.
    pub fn cache(&self) -> Result<Cache, IpcError> {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&self.cache_dir).map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
        Cache::open(&self.cache_dir.join(CACHE_FILE)).map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
    }
}

fn recent_views(settings: &LibrarySettings) -> Vec<RecentView> {
    settings
        .recent
        .iter()
        .map(|r| RecentView {
            path: r.path.display().to_string(),
            name: r.name.clone(),
            profile_id: r.profile_id.clone(),
            roms: r.roms,
            opened_at: r.opened_at,
            available: r.path.is_dir(),
        })
        .collect()
}

fn now_unix() -> i64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| i64::try_from(d.as_secs()).unwrap_or(i64::MAX))
        .unwrap_or(0)
}
```

```rust
//! Pins the webview's file access: no asset protocol, no fs plugin, no
//! opener permissions for JS, art only as blob URLs.

use serde_json::Value;

fn json(text: &str) -> Value {

#[test]
fn asset_protocol_stays_disabled_and_csp_allows_only_blob_images() {
    let conf = json(include_str!("../tauri.conf.json"));
    let security = &conf["app"]["security"];
    assert!(
        security.get("assetProtocol").is_none()
            || security["assetProtocol"]["enable"] == Value::Bool(false),
        "asset protocol must stay disabled: {security}"
    );

        .split(';')
        .map(str::trim)
        .find(|d| d.starts_with("img-src"))
        .unwrap();
```

```rust
//! IPC commands exposed to the webview. Each is a thin wrapper over
//! `state`/`library`; blocking work runs on the blocking thread pool.

use std::path::Path;

use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use serde::Serialize;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter, Manager, State};
use tauri_plugin_opener::OpenerExt;

use crate::error::IpcError;
use crate::library::art::{entry_path, read_art_bytes};
use crate::library::view::{LibrarySettingsView, LibraryView, RecentView};
use crate::state::AppState;

/// Product name shown in the UI and reported over IPC.
pub const APP_NAME: &str = "Coverly ROMs";
/// Event carrying `ScanProgressEvent` while a library opens.
pub const SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT: &str = "library-scan-progress";

/// Name and versions of the app and of the core library it is built on.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct AppInfo {
    pub name: String,
    pub version: String,
    pub core_version: String,
}

/// Who we are and which versions are running.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn app_info() -> AppInfo {
    AppInfo {
        name: APP_NAME.to_owned(),
        version: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION").to_owned(),
        core_version: coverly_core::VERSION.to_owned(),
    }
}

async fn blocking<T: Send + 'static>(
    f: impl FnOnce() -> Result<T, IpcError> + Send + 'static,
) -> Result<T, IpcError> {
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(f)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?
}

/// Opens a library folder, emitting `library-scan-progress` events.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn open_library(
    app: AppHandle,
    path: String,
    profile_id: Option<String>,
) -> Result<LibraryView, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || {
        let state = app.state::<AppState>();
        state.open(
            Path::new(&path),
            profile_id.as_deref(),
            &SystemDb::builtin(),
            &mut |event| {
                let _ = app.emit(SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT, event);
            },
        )
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn recent_libraries(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Vec<RecentView>, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || Ok(app.state::<AppState>().recent())).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn forget_recent_library(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    path: String,
) -> Result<Vec<RecentView>, IpcError> {
    state.forget(Path::new(&path))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn library_settings(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> LibrarySettingsView {
    state.library_settings()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn set_library_settings(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    ignored_dirs: Vec<String>,
) -> Result<LibrarySettingsView, IpcError> {
    state.set_ignored_dirs(ignored_dirs)
}

/// The raw bytes of one art image (an `ArrayBuffer` in JS).
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn read_art(
    app: AppHandle,
    generation: u64,
    entry_id: u32,
    media: Media,
) -> Result<tauri::ipc::Response, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || {
        let session = app.state::<AppState>().session(generation)?;
        read_art_bytes(&session, entry_id, media).map(tauri::ipc::Response::new)
    })
    .await
}

/// Shows the entry's file in Finder / Explorer / the file manager.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn reveal_entry(
    app: AppHandle,
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    generation: u64,
    entry_id: u32,
) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    let session = state.session(generation)?;
    let path = entry_path(&session, entry_id)?;
    app.opener()
        .reveal_item_in_dir(path)
        .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
}
```

Replace `run()` in `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` (keep its existing tests):

```rust
//! Tauri shell of Coverly ROMs: a thin IPC layer over `coverly-core`.

pub mod error;
pub mod ipc;
pub mod library;
pub mod state;

pub use ipc::{app_info, AppInfo, APP_NAME};

use coverly_core::paths::{config_dir, AppDirs};
use coverly_core::settings::LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE;
use tauri::Manager;

/// Builds and runs the Tauri application.
pub fn run() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_opener::init())
        .setup(|app| {
            let settings_path = config_dir()
                .ok_or("cannot find the app config folder")?
                .join(LIBRARY_SETTINGS_FILE);
            let cache_dir = AppDirs::resolve(None, None)
                .ok_or("cannot find the app cache folder")?
                .cache;
            app.manage(state::AppState::new(settings_path, cache_dir));
            Ok(())
        })
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            ipc::app_info,
            ipc::open_library,
            ipc::recent_libraries,
            ipc::forget_recent_library,
            ipc::library_settings,
            ipc::set_library_settings,
            ipc::read_art,
            ipc::reveal_entry,
        ])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running Coverly ROMs");
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use common::{anbernic_tree, open, write};
use coverly_core::cache::Cache;
use coverly_core::identify::names::Region;
use coverly_core::identify::IdMethod;
use coverly_core::net::{Http, HttpRequest, HttpResponse, NetError};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::library::identify::identify_entry;

/// The CRC32/SHA1 of the bytes `123456789`.
const DAT: &str = "clrmamepro (\n\tname \"Test\"\n)\n\ngame (\n\tname \"Check Game (USA)\"\n\trom ( name \"Check Game (USA).gba\" size 9 crc CBF43926 sha1 F7C3BC1D808E04732ADF679965CCC34CA7AE3441 )\n)\n";

struct DatHttp;
impl Http for DatHttp {
    fn get(&self, _: &HttpRequest) -> Result<HttpResponse, NetError> {
        Ok(HttpResponse {
            status: 200,
            etag: None,
            last_modified: None,
            content_type: None,
            retry_after: None,
            body: DAT.as_bytes().to_vec(),
        })
    }
}

struct OfflineHttp;
impl Http for OfflineHttp {
    fn get(&self, _: &HttpRequest) -> Result<HttpResponse, NetError> {
        Err(NetError::Offline("test".into()))
    }
}

const BASE: &str = "https://example.invalid/db";

fn library() -> (tempfile::TempDir, coverly_roms_lib::library::Session) {
    let tree = anbernic_tree(&["GBA"], 0, 0);
    write(tree.path(), "ROMS/GBA/Some Dump (USA).gba", b"123456789");
    let (session, _) = open(tree.path(), None).unwrap();
    (tree, session)
}

#[test]
fn identifies_by_hash_when_the_dat_is_reachable() {
    let (_tree, session) = library();
    let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
    let id = identify_entry(&session, 0, &SystemDb::builtin(), &cache, &DatHttp, BASE).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(id.canonical.as_deref(), Some("Check Game (USA)"));
    assert_eq!(id.method, IdMethod::Hash);
    assert_eq!(id.confidence, 1.0);
    assert_eq!(id.regions, vec![Region::Usa]);
    assert!(!id.offline);
}

#[test]
fn offline_without_cached_dats_falls_back_to_the_name() {
    let (_tree, session) = library();
    let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
    let id =
        identify_entry(&session, 0, &SystemDb::builtin(), &cache, &OfflineHttp, BASE).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(id.canonical, None);
    assert_eq!(id.method, IdMethod::Name);
    assert_eq!(id.confidence, 0.0);
    assert_eq!(id.title, "Some Dump");
    assert_eq!(id.regions, vec![Region::Usa]);
    assert!(id.offline);
}

#[test]
fn offline_with_a_cached_dat_still_identifies_by_hash() {
    let (_tree, session) = library();
    let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
    let db = SystemDb::builtin();
    identify_entry(&session, 0, &db, &cache, &DatHttp, BASE).unwrap();
    let id = identify_entry(&session, 0, &db, &cache, &OfflineHttp, BASE).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(id.method, IdMethod::Hash);
    assert!(!id.offline);
}

#[test]
fn unknown_entry_is_rejected() {
    let (_tree, session) = library();
    let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        identify_entry(&session, 42, &SystemDb::builtin(), &cache, &DatHttp, BASE).unwrap_err(),
        IpcError::UnknownEntry(42)
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src-tauri/src/library/identify.rs`:

```rust
//! Identifying one entry on demand with the core identifier. Offline-safe:
//! cached DATs (and the hash memo) answer without the network; when the
//! DATs are unreachable and not cached, the entry is identified by name and
//! the result says it was offline.

use coverly_core::cache::Cache;
use coverly_core::identify::dat::{DatError, DatStore};
use coverly_core::identify::names::{parse_name, Region};
use coverly_core::identify::{IdMethod, Identifier, IdentifyOptions};
use coverly_core::net::Http;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use serde::Serialize;

use super::Session;
use crate::error::IpcError;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct IdentityView {
    /// DAT or arcade name, when identified.
    pub canonical: Option<String>,
    pub method: IdMethod,
    /// 1.0 for a DAT hit, 0.0 otherwise.
    pub confidence: f32,
    /// Parsed title (of the canonical name, or else of the file name).
    pub title: String,
    pub regions: Vec<Region>,
    /// The DATs could not be reached and none was cached.
    pub offline: bool,
}

pub fn identify_entry(
    session: &Session,
    id: u32,
    systems: &SystemDb,
    cache: &Cache,
    http: &dyn Http,
    database_base: &str,
) -> Result<IdentityView, IpcError> {
    let entry = session
        .entries
        .get(id as usize)
        .ok_or(IpcError::UnknownEntry(id))?;
    let system = systems
        .get(&entry.system_id)
        .ok_or(IpcError::UnknownEntry(id))?;
    let dats = DatStore::new(http, cache, database_base);
    let identifier = Identifier::new(cache, &dats, IdentifyOptions::default());
    let (identity, offline) = match identifier.identify(&entry.rom, system, false) {
        Ok(identity) => (identity, false),
        Err(DatError::Cache(e)) => return Err(IpcError::Io(e.to_string())),
        Err(e) => {
            let offline = matches!(&e, DatError::Net(net) if net.pauses());
            (
                identifier.unidentified(parse_name(&entry.rom.stem, false)),
                offline,
            )
        }
    };
    Ok(IdentityView {
        canonical: identity.canonical,
        method: identity.method,
        confidence: identity.confidence,
        title: identity.parsed.title,
        regions: identity.parsed.regions,
        offline,
    })
}
```

Add `pub mod identify;` to `src-tauri/src/library/mod.rs`.

Add the command to `src-tauri/src/ipc.rs`:

```rust
/// Identifies one entry (hash → DAT, else name); never fails for lack of network.
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn identify_entry(
    app: AppHandle,
    generation: u64,
    entry_id: u32,
) -> Result<crate::library::identify::IdentityView, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || {
        let state = app.state::<AppState>();
        let session = state.session(generation)?;
        let cache = state.cache()?;
        let net = coverly_core::net::NetConfig::default();
        let http =
            coverly_core::net::PoliteHttp::new(coverly_core::net::UreqHttp::new(&net), &net);
        crate::library::identify::identify_entry(
            &session,
            entry_id,
            &SystemDb::builtin(),
            &cache,
            &http,
            coverly_core::identify::dat::DATABASE_BASE,
        )
    })
    .await
}
```

and `ipc::identify_entry` to `generate_handler!` in `lib.rs`.

```ts
export interface Backend {
  readonly kind: 'tauri' | 'mock';
  openLibrary(path: string, profileId?: string): Promise<LibraryView>;
  onScanProgress(cb: (p: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<() => void>;
  recentLibraries(): Promise<RecentLibrary[]>;
  forgetRecentLibrary(path: string): Promise<RecentLibrary[]>;
  librarySettings(): Promise<LibrarySettings>;
  setLibrarySettings(s: LibrarySettings): Promise<LibrarySettings>;
  readArt(generation: number, entryId: number, media: Media): Promise<ArtImage>;
  identifyEntry(generation: number, entryId: number): Promise<IdentityView>;
  revealEntry(generation: number, entryId: number): Promise<void>;
  pickFolder(): Promise<string | null>;
  onFolderDrop(cb: (path: string) => void): Promise<() => void>;
}
```

```rust
//! The wire contract shared with `src/ipc/types.ts`: Rust must serialise
//! exactly `src/ipc/contract.sample.json`.

use coverly_core::identify::names::Region;
use coverly_core::identify::IdMethod;
```
