```rust
    ArtMark, ArtMarks, EntryView, LibrarySettingsView, LibraryView, ProfileView, RecentView,
    ScanProgressEvent, SystemView, WarningView,
};
use serde_json::{json, Value};

#[test]
fn rust_serialises_the_shared_sample() {
    let view = LibraryView {
        generation: 3,
        root: "/Volumes/SD".into(),
        name: "SD".into(),
        profile: ProfileView {
            id: "anbernic-stock".into(),
            name: "Anbernic stock".into(),
            media: vec![Media::Cover],
            detected: true,
            evidence: vec!["anbernic/ folder".into()],
            fitted: false,

        systems: vec![SystemView {
            index: 0,
            folder: "SFC".into(),
            dir: "ROMS/SFC".into(),

        }],
        entries: vec![EntryView {
            id: 0,
            system: 0,
            rel_path: "ROMS/SFC/Zelda (USA).sfc".into(),
            dir: "ROMS/SFC".into(),
            name: "Zelda (USA)".into(),
            ext: "sfc".into(),
            size: 1_048_576,
            mtime: 1_700_000_000,
            regions: vec![Region::Usa],
            art: ArtMarks {
                cover: ArtMark::Present,
                screenshot: ArtMark::Unsupported,
                title: ArtMark::Unsupported,
            },
        }],
        warnings: vec![WarningView {
            path: "/Volumes/SD/ROMS/BAD".into(),
            message: "permission denied".into(),
        }],
        scan_ms: 42,
    };
    let actual = json!({
        "library_view": view,
        "scan_progress": ScanProgressEvent { generation: 3, done: 1, total: 2, folder: "SFC".into(), roms: 1 },
        "recent": RecentView {
            path: "/Volumes/SD".into(),
            name: "SD".into(),
            profile_id: "anbernic-stock".into(),
            roms: 1,
            opened_at: 1_700_000_000,

        "settings": LibrarySettingsView { ignored_dirs: vec!["bios".into()] },
        "identity": IdentityView {
            canonical: Some("Legend of Zelda, The (USA)".into()),
            method: IdMethod::Hash,
            confidence: 1.0,
            title: "Legend of Zelda, The".into(),
            regions: vec![Region::Usa],
            offline: false,
        },
        "error": IpcError::NotFound("/Volumes/SD".into()),
    });
    let expected: Value =
        serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../src/ipc/contract.sample.json")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(actual, expected);
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import sample from './contract.sample.json';
import type {
  EntryView,
  IdentityView,
  LibraryView,
  ProfileView,
  RecentLibrary,
  ScanProgress,
  SystemView,
} from './types';

/** Compiles only when `K` lists every key of `T`. */
type AllKeys<T, K extends readonly (keyof T)[]> =
  Exclude<keyof T, K[number]> extends never ? K : never;

const VIEW_KEYS = [
  'generation',
  'root',
  'name',
  'profile',
  'systems',
  'entries',
  'warnings',
  'scan_ms',
] as const;
const ENTRY_KEYS = [
  'id',
  'system',
  'rel_path',
  'dir',
  'name',
  'ext',
  'size',
  'mtime',
  'regions',
  'art',
] as const;
const SYSTEM_KEYS = [
  'index',
  'folder',
  'dir',
  'system_id',
  'name',
  'roms',
] as const;
const PROFILE_KEYS = [
  'id',
  'name',
  'media',
  'detected',
  'evidence',
  'fitted',
] as const;
const PROGRESS_KEYS = [
  'generation',
  'done',
  'total',
  'folder',
  'roms',
] as const;
const RECENT_KEYS = [
  'path',
  'name',
  'profile_id',
  'roms',
  'opened_at',
  'available',
] as const;
const IDENTITY_KEYS = [
  'canonical',
  'method',
  'confidence',
  'title',
  'regions',
  'offline',
] as const;

const checks: [string, readonly string[], object][] = [
  [
    'LibraryView',
    VIEW_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<LibraryView, typeof VIEW_KEYS>,
    sample.library_view,
  ],
  [
    'EntryView',
    ENTRY_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<EntryView, typeof ENTRY_KEYS>,
    sample.library_view.entries[0]!,
  ],
  [
    'SystemView',
    SYSTEM_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<SystemView, typeof SYSTEM_KEYS>,
    sample.library_view.systems[0]!,
  ],
  [
    'ProfileView',
    PROFILE_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<ProfileView, typeof PROFILE_KEYS>,
    sample.library_view.profile,
  ],
  [
    'ScanProgress',
    PROGRESS_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<ScanProgress, typeof PROGRESS_KEYS>,
    sample.scan_progress,
  ],
  [
    'RecentLibrary',
    RECENT_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<RecentLibrary, typeof RECENT_KEYS>,
    sample.recent,
  ],
  [
    'IdentityView',
    IDENTITY_KEYS satisfies AllKeys<IdentityView, typeof IDENTITY_KEYS>,
    sample.identity,
  ],
];

describe('wire contract', () => {
  it.each(checks)('%s keys match the Rust sample', (_name, keys, value) => {
    expect(Object.keys(value).sort()).toEqual([...keys].sort());
  });

  it('art marks and media use the Rust spellings', () => {
    expect(sample.library_view.entries[0]!.art).toEqual({
      cover: 'present',
      screenshot: 'unsupported',
      title: 'unsupported',
    });
    expect(sample.library_view.profile.media).toEqual(['cover']);
  });
});
```

`src/ipc/errors.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { translate } from '@/i18n/core';

import { BackendError, ERROR_CODES, errorKey, toBackendError } from './errors';

describe('backend errors', () => {
  it('maps the Rust wire shape', () => {
    const e = toBackendError({ code: 'not-found', detail: '/x' });
    expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(BackendError);
    expect(e.code).toBe('not-found');
    expect(e.detail).toBe('/x');
  });

  it('keeps numeric details as text and unit variants without detail', () => {
    expect(toBackendError({ code: 'unknown-entry', detail: 5 }).detail).toBe(
      '5',
    );
    expect(toBackendError({ code: 'no-library' }).detail).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('turns anything else into an unknown error', () => {
    expect(toBackendError('boom').code).toBe('unknown');
    expect(toBackendError(new Error('bad')).detail).toBe('bad');
    expect(toBackendError({ code: 'made-up' }).code).toBe('unknown');
  });

  it.each(ERROR_CODES)('%s has a message in both languages', (code) => {
    expect(translate('en', errorKey(code))).not.toBe(errorKey(code));
    expect(translate('es', errorKey(code))).not.toBe(errorKey(code));
  });
});
```

`src/ipc/sniff.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { sniffImageMime } from './sniff';

const bytes = (...b: number[]) => new Uint8Array(b);
const ascii = (s: string) => new TextEncoder().encode(s);

describe('sniffImageMime', () => {
  it('recognises PNG, JPEG, WebP and GIF', () => {
    expect(
      sniffImageMime(bytes(0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a)),
    ).toBe('image/png');
    expect(sniffImageMime(bytes(0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xe0))).toBe('image/jpeg');
    expect(sniffImageMime(ascii('RIFF\0\0\0\0WEBPVP8 '))).toBe('image/webp');
    expect(sniffImageMime(ascii('GIF89a'))).toBe('image/gif');
  });

  it('refuses SVG, HTML and short input', () => {
    expect(sniffImageMime(ascii('<svg onload=alert(1)>'))).toBeNull();
    expect(sniffImageMime(ascii('<html>'))).toBeNull();
    expect(sniffImageMime(bytes(0x89))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { clearMocks, mockIPC } from '@tauri-apps/api/mocks';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import sample from './contract.sample.json';
import { BackendError } from './errors';

const dialog = vi.hoisted(() => ({ open: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock('@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog', () => dialog);

const events = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  handlers: new Map<string, (e: { payload: unknown }) => void>(),
}));
// Keep the module's other exports: `@tauri-apps/api/webview` imports them.
vi.mock('@tauri-apps/api/event', async (importOriginal) => ({
  ...(await importOriginal<typeof import('@tauri-apps/api/event')>()),
  listen: vi.fn(
    async (name: string, cb: (e: { payload: unknown }) => void) => {
      events.handlers.set(name, cb);
      return () => events.handlers.delete(name);
    },
  ),
}));

import { createTauriBackend, SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT } from './tauriBackend';

const PNG = new Uint8Array([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]);

describe('Tauri backend', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    clearMocks();
    dialog.open.mockReset();
  });

  it('opens a library with camelCase arguments', async () => {
    const calls: [string, unknown][] = [];
    mockIPC((cmd, args) => {
      calls.push([cmd, args]);
      return sample.library_view;
    });
    const view = await createTauriBackend().openLibrary('/Volumes/SD');
    expect(view.entries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      ['open_library', { path: '/Volumes/SD', profileId: null }],
    ]);
  });

  it('passes entry ids and media to read_art and sniffs the bytes', async () => {
    const calls: [string, unknown][] = [];
    mockIPC((cmd, args) => {
      calls.push([cmd, args]);
      return PNG.buffer;
    });
    const art = await createTauriBackend().readArt(3, 9, 'cover');
    expect(art.mime).toBe('image/png');
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      ['read_art', { generation: 3, entryId: 9, media: 'cover' }],
    ]);
  });

  it('refuses bytes that are not an image', async () => {
    mockIPC(() => new TextEncoder().encode('<svg/>').buffer);
    await expect(
      createTauriBackend().readArt(1, 0, 'cover'),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'art-unreadable' });
  });

  it('maps command errors to BackendError', async () => {
    mockIPC(() => Promise.reject({ code: 'not-found', detail: '/x' }));
    const err = await createTauriBackend()
      .openLibrary('/x')
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(BackendError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ code: 'not-found', detail: '/x' });
  });

  it('sends ignored folders as ignoredDirs', async () => {
    const calls: [string, unknown][] = [];
    mockIPC((cmd, args) => {
      calls.push([cmd, args]);
      return { ignored_dirs: ['bios'] };
    });
    await createTauriBackend().setLibrarySettings({ ignored_dirs: ['bios'] });
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      ['set_library_settings', { ignoredDirs: ['bios'] }],
    ]);
  });

  it('forwards scan progress events', async () => {
    const seen: unknown[] = [];
    const off = await createTauriBackend().onScanProgress((p) => seen.push(p));
    events.handlers.get(SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT)!({ payload: sample.scan_progress });
    expect(seen).toEqual([sample.scan_progress]);
    off();
    expect(events.handlers.has(SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('picks a single folder with the dialog', async () => {
    dialog.open.mockResolvedValueOnce('/picked');
    await expect(createTauriBackend().pickFolder()).resolves.toBe('/picked');
    expect(dialog.open).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      directory: true,
      multiple: false,
    });
    dialog.open.mockResolvedValueOnce(null);
    await expect(createTauriBackend().pickFolder()).resolves.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/ipc/errors.ts`:

```ts
/** Backend failures: the Rust `IpcError` wire shape, localised by code. */

export const ERROR_CODES = [
  'not-found',
  'not-a-folder',
  'unreadable',
  'no-profile',
  'unknown-profile',
  'no-library',
  'stale-library',
  'superseded',
  'unknown-entry',
  'art-missing',
  'outside-library',
  'art-too-large',
  'io',
  'settings',
  'art-unreadable',
  'unknown',
] as const;

export type ErrorCode = (typeof ERROR_CODES)[number];

export class BackendError extends Error {
  readonly code: ErrorCode;
  readonly detail: string | undefined;

  constructor(code: ErrorCode, detail?: string) {
    super(detail ? `${code}: ${detail}` : code);
    this.name = 'BackendError';
    this.code = code;
    this.detail = detail;
  }
}

function isErrorCode(value: unknown): value is ErrorCode {
  return (
    typeof value === 'string' &&
    (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  );
}

export function toBackendError(e: unknown): BackendError {
  if (e instanceof BackendError) return e;
  if (typeof e === 'object' && e !== null && 'code' in e) {
    const { code, detail } = e as { code: unknown; detail?: unknown };
    if (isErrorCode(code)) {
      return new BackendError(
        code,
        detail === undefined || detail === null ? undefined : String(detail),
      );
    }
  }
  return new BackendError('unknown', e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
}

/** The i18n key of a backend error. */
export function errorKey(code: ErrorCode): string {
  return `errors.${code}`;
}
```

`src/ipc/sniff.ts`:

```ts
/** The MIME type of PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF bytes, or null (SVG is never accepted). */
export function sniffImageMime(b: Uint8Array): string | null {
  const ascii = (from: number, to: number) =>
    String.fromCharCode(...b.subarray(from, to));
  if (b.length >= 8 && b[0] === 0x89 && ascii(1, 4) === 'PNG') {
    return 'image/png';
  }
  if (b.length >= 3 && b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xd8 && b[2] === 0xff) {
    return 'image/jpeg';
  }
  if (b.length >= 12 && ascii(0, 4) === 'RIFF' && ascii(8, 12) === 'WEBP') {
    return 'image/webp';
  }
  if (b.length >= 6 && ascii(0, 3) === 'GIF') return 'image/gif';
  return null;
}
```

```ts
  onScanProgress(cb: (p: ScanProgress) => void): Promise<() => void>;
  recentLibraries(): Promise<RecentLibrary[]>;
  forgetRecentLibrary(path: string): Promise<RecentLibrary[]>;
  librarySettings(): Promise<LibrarySettings>;
  setLibrarySettings(s: LibrarySettings): Promise<LibrarySettings>;

  identifyEntry(generation: number, entryId: number): Promise<IdentityView>;
```

`src/ipc/tauriBackend.ts`:

```ts
import { invoke } from '@tauri-apps/api/core';
import { listen } from '@tauri-apps/api/event';
import { getCurrentWebview } from '@tauri-apps/api/webview';
import { open } from '@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog';

import type { Backend } from './backend';
import { BackendError, toBackendError } from './errors';
import { sniffImageMime } from './sniff';
import type {
  IdentityView,
  LibrarySettings,
  LibraryView,
  RecentLibrary,
  ScanProgress,
} from './types';

export const SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT = 'library-scan-progress';

async function call<T>(cmd: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await invoke<T>(cmd, args);
  } catch (e) {
    throw toBackendError(e);
  }
}

export function createTauriBackend(): Backend {
  return {
    kind: 'tauri',
    openLibrary: (path, profileId) =>
      call<LibraryView>('open_library', { path, profileId: profileId ?? null }),
    onScanProgress: (cb) =>
      listen<ScanProgress>(SCAN_PROGRESS_EVENT, (e) => cb(e.payload)),
    recentLibraries: () => call<RecentLibrary[]>('recent_libraries'),
    forgetRecentLibrary: (path) =>
      call<RecentLibrary[]>('forget_recent_library', { path }),
    librarySettings: () => call<LibrarySettings>('library_settings'),
    setLibrarySettings: (s) =>
      call<LibrarySettings>('set_library_settings', {
        ignoredDirs: s.ignored_dirs,
      }),
    async readArt(generation, entryId, media) {
      const buffer = await call<ArrayBuffer>('read_art', {
        generation,
        entryId,
        media,
      });
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
      const mime = sniffImageMime(bytes);
      if (!mime) throw new BackendError('art-unreadable');
      return { bytes, mime };
    },
    identifyEntry: (generation, entryId) =>
      call<IdentityView>('identify_entry', { generation, entryId }),
    revealEntry: (generation, entryId) =>
      call<void>('reveal_entry', { generation, entryId }),
    async pickFolder() {
      const picked = await open({ directory: true, multiple: false });
      return typeof picked === 'string' ? picked : null;
    },
    onFolderDrop: (cb) =>
      getCurrentWebview().onDragDropEvent((event) => {
        if (event.payload.type !== 'drop') return;
        const first = event.payload.paths[0];
        if (first) cb(first);
      }),
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import {
  FIXTURE_NOW,
  generateLibrary,
  HOSTILE_NAMES,
  mulberry32,
} from './fixture';

const make = (count: number, seed = 12) =>
  generateLibrary({
    seed,
    count,
    root: '/mock/Fixture',
    generation: 1,
    now: FIXTURE_NOW,
  });

describe('fixture generator', () => {
  it('is deterministic for a seed', () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(make(500))).toBe(JSON.stringify(make(500)));
    expect(JSON.stringify(make(500, 1))).not.toBe(JSON.stringify(make(500)));
    const a = mulberry32(3);
    const b = mulberry32(3);
    expect([a(), a(), a()]).toEqual([b(), b(), b()]);
  });

  it('produces exactly 12,000 well-formed entries quickly', () => {
    const started = performance.now();
    const view = make(12_000);
    expect(performance.now() - started).toBeLessThan(1500);
    expect(view.entries).toHaveLength(12_000);
    view.entries.forEach((e, i) => {
      expect(e.id).toBe(i);
      expect(view.systems[e.system]).toBeDefined();
      expect(e.rel_path.endsWith(`${e.name}.${e.ext}`)).toBe(true);
      expect(e.rel_path.startsWith(`${e.dir}/`)).toBe(true);
    });
    expect(view.systems.reduce((n, s) => n + s.roms, 0)).toBe(12_000);
    expect(new Set(view.entries.map((e) => e.rel_path)).size).toBe(12_000);
  });

  it('mixes art states for every media', () => {
    const view = make(12_000);
    for (const media of ['cover', 'screenshot', 'title'] as const) {
      const present = view.entries.filter(
        (e) => e.art[media] === 'present',
      ).length;
      expect(present / 12_000).toBeGreaterThan(0.2);
      expect(present / 12_000).toBeLessThan(0.8);
    }
    expect(view.profile.media).toEqual(['cover', 'screenshot', 'title']);
  });

  it('spreads modification dates and includes recent ones', () => {
    const view = make(12_000);
    const week = view.entries.filter((e) => FIXTURE_NOW - e.mtime < 7 * 86_400);
    const old = view.entries.filter(
      (e) => FIXTURE_NOW - e.mtime > 365 * 86_400,
    );
    expect(week.length).toBeGreaterThan(50);
    expect(old.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
  });

  it('includes the hostile names verbatim', () => {
    const names = make(240).entries.map((e) => e.name);
    for (const hostile of HOSTILE_NAMES) expect(names).toContain(hostile);
  });

  it('can be empty', () => {
    const view = make(0);
    expect(view.entries).toEqual([]);
    expect(view.systems.every((s) => s.roms === 0)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`src/ipc/mock/fixture.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Deterministic fake libraries for the browser mock and the tests: the
 * same seed always gives the same LibraryView (names, sizes, dates, art).
 */
import type {
  ArtMark,
  EntryView,
  LibraryView,
  Region,
  SystemView,
} from '../types';

/** 2026-10-03T00:13:20Z: the `now` of the unit tests. */
export const FIXTURE_NOW = 1_791_000_000;
const DAY = 86_400;

/** Small, fast, seedable PRNG returning floats in [0, 1). */
export function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4_294_967_296;
  };
}

interface FixtureSystem {
  folder: string;
  id: string;
  name: string;
  exts: readonly string[];
  weight: number;
}

export const FIXTURE_SYSTEMS: readonly FixtureSystem[] = [
  { folder: 'DREAMCAST', id: 'dreamcast', name: 'Dreamcast', exts: ['chd', 'gdi'], weight: 2 },
  { folder: 'FC', id: 'nes', name: 'NES / Famicom', exts: ['nes'], weight: 10 },
  { folder: 'GB', id: 'gb', name: 'Game Boy', exts: ['gb'], weight: 6 },
  { folder: 'GBA', id: 'gba', name: 'Game Boy Advance', exts: ['gba', 'zip'], weight: 9 },
  { folder: 'GBC', id: 'gbc', name: 'Game Boy Color', exts: ['gbc'], weight: 5 },
  { folder: 'GG', id: 'gamegear', name: 'Game Gear', exts: ['gg'], weight: 3 },
  { folder: 'LYNX', id: 'lynx', name: 'Atari Lynx', exts: ['lnx'], weight: 1 },
  { folder: 'MAME', id: 'mame', name: 'MAME', exts: ['zip'], weight: 8 },
  { folder: 'MD', id: 'megadrive', name: 'Mega Drive', exts: ['md', 'bin', 'gen'], weight: 8 },
  { folder: 'N64', id: 'n64', name: 'Nintendo 64', exts: ['z64', 'n64'], weight: 4 },
  { folder: 'NDS', id: 'nds', name: 'Nintendo DS', exts: ['nds'], weight: 5 },
  { folder: 'NEOGEO', id: 'neogeo', name: 'Neo Geo', exts: ['zip'], weight: 2 },
  { folder: 'PCE', id: 'pcengine', name: 'PC Engine', exts: ['pce'], weight: 3 },
  { folder: 'PS', id: 'psx', name: 'PlayStation', exts: ['pbp', 'chd', 'm3u'], weight: 7 },
  { folder: 'PSP', id: 'psp', name: 'PSP', exts: ['iso', 'cso'], weight: 3 },
  { folder: 'SFC', id: 'snes', name: 'Super Nintendo', exts: ['sfc', 'smc'], weight: 10 },
  { folder: 'SMS', id: 'mastersystem', name: 'Master System', exts: ['sms'], weight: 3 },
  { folder: 'WS', id: 'wonderswan', name: 'WonderSwan', exts: ['ws'], weight: 1 },
];

/** Names that must render as text, never as markup (in SFC). */
export const HOSTILE_NAMES = [
  '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>',
  'Pokémon - Edición Oro (Spain)',
  '..\\..\\escape',
  '‮gnp.exe',
  `${'Very Long Title '.repeat(11).trim()} (USA)`,
] as const;

const HOSTILE_REGIONS: Record<string, Region[]> = {
  'Pokémon - Edición Oro (Spain)': ['spain'],
};

const FIRST = [
  'Super', 'Mega', 'Final', 'Legend of', 'Street', 'Dragon', 'Metal', 'Sonic',
  'Star', 'Shadow', 'Castle', 'Ninja', 'Puzzle', 'Turbo', 'Space', 'Pokémon',
  'Crónicas', 'Æther', 'Kirby', 'Mortal',
];
const SECOND = [
  'Fighter', 'Quest', 'Warriors', 'Adventure', 'Racing', 'Soccer', 'Kombat',
  'Hunter', 'Island', 'Saga', 'Force', 'Tactics', 'Dreams', 'Blaster',
  'Odyssey', 'Knights', 'Heroes', 'Rally', 'Tennis', 'Galaxy',
];
const SUFFIX = ['', '', '', ' II', ' III', ' 64', ' Advance', ' Deluxe', ': The Return', ' Turbo'];
const TAGS: readonly { tag: string; regions: Region[] }[] = [
  { tag: ' (USA)', regions: ['usa'] },
  { tag: ' (Europe)', regions: ['europe'] },
  { tag: ' (Japan)', regions: ['japan'] },
  { tag: ' (USA, Europe)', regions: ['usa', 'europe'] },
  { tag: ' (Spain)', regions: ['spain'] },
  { tag: ' (World)', regions: ['world'] },
  { tag: '', regions: [] },
];

export interface FixtureOptions {
  seed: number;
  count: number;
  /** Shown as the library root; its last segment is the library name. */
  root: string;
  generation: number;
  /** Unix seconds that "recent" dates are relative to. */
  now: number;
}

const collator = new Intl.Collator('en', { sensitivity: 'base', numeric: true });

export function generateLibrary(o: FixtureOptions): LibraryView {
  const rand = mulberry32(o.seed);
  const pick = <T>(items: readonly T[]): T =>
    items[Math.floor(rand() * items.length)] as T;
  const mark = (p: number): ArtMark => (rand() < p ? 'present' : 'missing');

  const totalWeight = FIXTURE_SYSTEMS.reduce((n, s) => n + s.weight, 0);
  const counts = FIXTURE_SYSTEMS.map((s) =>
    Math.floor((o.count * s.weight) / totalWeight),
  );
  let rest = o.count - counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  for (let i = 0; rest > 0; i = (i + 1) % counts.length, rest -= 1) {
    counts[i] = (counts[i] ?? 0) + 1;
  }

  const systems: SystemView[] = [];
  const entries: EntryView[] = [];
  FIXTURE_SYSTEMS.forEach((sys, index) => {
    const n = counts[index] ?? 0;
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const draft: Omit<EntryView, 'id'>[] = [];
    const base = `ROMS/${sys.folder}`;
    const hostile = sys.folder === 'SFC' && n >= HOSTILE_NAMES.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
      const sub = rand();
      const dir =
        sub < 0.08 ? `${base}/Hacks` : sub < 0.13 ? `${base}/Translations/ES` : base;
      const ext = pick(sys.exts);
      const tag = pick(TAGS);
      let name: string;
      let regions: Region[];
      if (hostile && i < HOSTILE_NAMES.length) {
        name = HOSTILE_NAMES[i] as string;
        regions = HOSTILE_REGIONS[name] ?? (name.endsWith('(USA)') ? ['usa'] : []);
      } else {
        name = `${pick(FIRST)} ${pick(SECOND)}${pick(SUFFIX)}${tag.tag}`;
        regions = tag.regions;
      }
      let unique = name;
      for (let k = 2; seen.has(`${dir}/${unique}.${ext}`); k += 1) {
        unique = `${name} (Rev ${k})`;
      }
      seen.add(`${dir}/${unique}.${ext}`);
      const recent = rand() < 0.03;
      const age = recent
        ? Math.floor(rand() * 6 * DAY)
        : Math.floor(rand() * 3 * 365 * DAY);
      draft.push({
        system: index,
        rel_path: `${dir}/${unique}.${ext}`,
        dir,
        name: unique,
        ext,
        size: Math.round(65_536 * Math.pow(10_000, rand())),
        mtime: o.now - age,
        regions,
        art: { cover: mark(0.62), screenshot: mark(0.45), title: mark(0.35) },
      });
    }
    draft.sort(
      (a, b) =>
        a.dir.localeCompare(b.dir) || collator.compare(a.name, b.name),
    );
    for (const d of draft) entries.push({ id: entries.length, ...d });
    systems.push({
      index,
      folder: sys.folder,
      dir: base,
      system_id: sys.id,
      name: sys.name,
      roms: n,
    });
  });

  const name = o.root.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop() ?? o.root;
  return {
    generation: o.generation,
    root: o.root,
    name,
    profile: {
      id: 'esde',
      name: 'ES-DE',
      media: ['cover', 'screenshot', 'title'],
      detected: true,
      evidence: ['mock fixture'],
      fitted: false,
    },
    systems,
    entries,
    warnings: [],
    scan_ms: 0,
  };
}
```

(Prettier will reflow `FIXTURE_SYSTEMS` and the word lists; run `pnpm format` after writing.)

Note: the hostile entries are the first `HOSTILE_NAMES.length` drafts of SFC and keep their place after the sort only by name order — the test checks presence, not position.

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the mock backend**

`src/ipc/mock/mockBackend.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Browser stand-in for the Tauri backend (`pnpm dev` in Chrome, tests):
 * deterministic fixture libraries, fake progress, SVG art, fake identity.
 */
import type { Backend } from '../backend';
import { BackendError } from '../errors';
import type {
  EntryView,
  LibrarySettings,
  LibraryView,
  Media,
  RecentLibrary,
  ScanProgress,
} from '../types';
import { generateLibrary } from './fixture';

export const MOCK_LIBRARIES = [
  { path: '/mock/Fixture SD (12k)', count: 12_000, seed: 12 },
  { path: '/mock/Small library', count: 240, seed: 7 },
  { path: '/mock/Empty library', count: 0, seed: 1 },
] as const;
export const MOCK_EJECTED = '/Volumes/EJECTED';

export interface MockOptions {
  /** Delay per scanned system, in ms (0 in tests). */
  delayMs?: number;
  /** Unix seconds that fixture dates are relative to. */
  now?: number;
}

export interface MockBackend extends Backend {
  /** Entry ids passed to `revealEntry`, for tests. */
  readonly revealed: number[];
}

const XML: Record<string, string> = {
  '&': '&amp;',
  '<': '&lt;',
  '>': '&gt;',
  '"': '&quot;',
  "'": '&apos;',
};

export function escapeXml(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => XML[c] ?? c);
}

export function mockArtSvg(e: EntryView, media: Media): string {
  const hue = (e.id * 47) % 360;
  const [w, h] = media === 'cover' ? [250, 360] : [320, 240];
  const label = escapeXml(
    e.name.length > 40 ? `${e.name.slice(0, 39)}…` : e.name,
  );
  return (
    `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${w}" height="${h}" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}">` +
    `<rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="hsl(${hue} 55% 42%)"/>` +
    `<text x="50%" y="50%" fill="#fff" font-family="system-ui" font-size="16" text-anchor="middle">${label}</text>` +
    `<text x="50%" y="${h - 16}" fill="#fff" opacity=".7" font-family="system-ui" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle">${media}</text>` +
    `</svg>`
  );
}

function normaliseDirs(dirs: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const raw of dirs) {
    const d = raw.trim();
    if (!d || d === '.' || d === '..' || /[\\/]/.test(d)) continue;
    if (out.some((o) => o.toLowerCase() === d.toLowerCase())) continue;
    out.push(d);
  }
  return out;
}

const DAY = 86_400;

export function createMockBackend(opts: MockOptions = {}): MockBackend {
  const delayMs = opts.delayMs ?? 30;
  const now = opts.now ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const sleep = (ms: number) =>
    ms > 0 ? new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)) : Promise.resolve();
  const listeners = new Set<(p: ScanProgress) => void>();
  const revealed: number[] = [];
  let generation = 0;
  let current: LibraryView | null = null;
  let settings: LibrarySettings = {
    ignored_dirs: ['bios', 'saves', 'states', 'Shortcuts'],
  };
  let recents: RecentLibrary[] = [
    ...MOCK_LIBRARIES.map((l, i) => ({
      path: l.path,
      name: l.path.split('/').pop() ?? l.path,
      profile_id: 'esde',
      roms: l.count,
      opened_at: now - i * DAY,
      available: true,
    })),
    {
      path: MOCK_EJECTED,
      name: 'EJECTED',
      profile_id: 'anbernic-stock',
      roms: 7473,
      opened_at: now - 9 * DAY,
      available: false,
    },
  ];

  const entryOf = (gen: number, id: number): EntryView => {
    if (!current) throw new BackendError('no-library');
    if (current.generation !== gen) throw new BackendError('stale-library');
    const e = current.entries[id];
    if (!e) throw new BackendError('unknown-entry', String(id));
    return e;
  };

  return {
    kind: 'mock',
    revealed,
    async openLibrary(path) {
      const gen = (generation += 1);
      const lib = MOCK_LIBRARIES.find((l) => l.path === path);
      if (!lib) throw new BackendError('not-found', path);
      const view = generateLibrary({
        seed: lib.seed,
        count: lib.count,
        root: lib.path,
        generation: gen,
        now,
      });
      let roms = 0;
      const emit = (p: Omit<ScanProgress, 'generation'>) =>
        listeners.forEach((cb) => cb({ generation: gen, ...p }));
      emit({ done: 0, total: view.systems.length, folder: '', roms: 0 });
      for (const [i, s] of view.systems.entries()) {
        await sleep(delayMs);
        roms += s.roms;
        emit({ done: i + 1, total: view.systems.length, folder: s.folder, roms });
      }
      if (gen !== generation) throw new BackendError('superseded');
      current = view;
      recents = [
        {
          path,
          name: view.name,
          profile_id: view.profile.id,
          roms: view.entries.length,
          opened_at: now,
          available: true,
        },
        ...recents.filter((r) => r.path !== path),
      ];
      return view;
    },
    onScanProgress(cb) {
      listeners.add(cb);
      return Promise.resolve(() => {
        listeners.delete(cb);
      });
    },
    recentLibraries: () => Promise.resolve([...recents]),
    forgetRecentLibrary(path) {
      recents = recents.filter((r) => r.path !== path);
      return Promise.resolve([...recents]);
    },
    librarySettings: () => Promise.resolve({ ...settings }),
    setLibrarySettings(s) {
      settings = { ignored_dirs: normaliseDirs(s.ignored_dirs) };
      return Promise.resolve({ ...settings });
    },
    async readArt(gen, id, media) {
      const e = entryOf(gen, id);
      if (e.art[media] !== 'present') {
        throw new BackendError('art-missing', media);
      }
      return {
        bytes: new TextEncoder().encode(mockArtSvg(e, media)),
        mime: 'image/svg+xml',
      };
    },
    async identifyEntry(gen, id) {
      const e = entryOf(gen, id);
      await sleep(delayMs);
      const title = e.name.replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)/g, '').trim();
      return e.id % 3 === 0
        ? {
            canonical: `${title} (USA)`,
            method: 'hash',
            confidence: 1,
            title,
            regions: ['usa'],
            offline: false,
          }
        : {
            canonical: null,
            method: 'name',
            confidence: 0,
            title,
            regions: e.regions,
            offline: e.id % 3 === 2,
          };
    },
    async revealEntry(gen, id) {
      entryOf(gen, id);
      revealed.push(id);
    },
    pickFolder: () => Promise.resolve(MOCK_LIBRARIES[0].path),
    onFolderDrop(cb) {
      const over = (ev: Event) => ev.preventDefault();
      const drop = (ev: Event) => {
        ev.preventDefault();
        const dt = (ev as DragEvent).dataTransfer;
        const name = dt?.files[0]?.name || dt?.getData('text/plain') || '';
        if (name) cb(name.startsWith('/') ? name : `/mock/${name}`);
      };
      window.addEventListener('dragover', over);
      window.addEventListener('drop', drop);
      return Promise.resolve(() => {
        window.removeEventListener('dragover', over);
        window.removeEventListener('drop', drop);
      });
    },
  };
}
```

Run: `cd /Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/coverly-roms && pnpm test -- src/ipc/backend.test.ts` → FAIL (`getBackend` is not exported). Then append to `src/ipc/backend.ts` (and add `import { hasTauriIpc } from './appInfo';` at the top):

```ts
let current: Backend | null = null;
let pending: Promise<Backend> | null = null;

async function createBackend(): Promise<Backend> {
  if (hasTauriIpc()) {
    const { createTauriBackend } = await import('./tauriBackend');
    return createTauriBackend();
  }
  const { createMockBackend } = await import('./mock/mockBackend');
  return createMockBackend();
}

/**
 * The Tauri backend inside the app, the mock in a plain browser. One
 * instance per page: concurrent first calls share the same promise (the
 * mock is stateful).
 */
export function getBackend(): Promise<Backend> {
  if (current) return Promise.resolve(current);
  pending ??= createBackend().then((backend) => {
    current ??= backend;
    pending = null;
    return current;
  });
  return pending;
}

/** Tests (and only tests) install a backend; `null` resets the choice. */
export function setBackend(backend: Backend | null): void {
  current = backend;
  pending = null;
}
```

Both adapters are dynamic imports, so `pnpm build` emits the mock as its own chunk that the Tauri app never loads.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { FIXTURE_NOW, generateLibrary } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';

import {
  activeFilterCount,
  applyFilters,
  computeFacets,

describe('art filters', () => {

describe('applyFilters', () => {

  ];
  const keys = buildSearchKeys(entries);
  const ids = (filters: Filters) => applyFilters(entries, keys, filters).map((x) => x.id);

  it('searches accent- and case-insensitively', () => {
    expect(ids(f({ query: 'POKEMON' }))).toEqual([0]);
    expect(ids(f({ query: 'pok oro' }))).toEqual([0]);
  });

  it('combines every filter', () => {
    expect(ids(f({ systems: [1] }))).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(ids(f({ systems: [1], art: 'missing-cover' }))).toEqual([1]);
    expect(ids(f({ exts: ['smc', 'gb'] }))).toEqual([0, 2]);
    expect(ids(f({ regions: ['europe', 'spain'] }))).toEqual([0, 2]);
    expect(ids(f({ modifiedFrom: 150, modifiedTo: 300 }))).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(ids(f({ sizeMin: 1000, sizeMax: 10_000 }))).toEqual([1]);
    expect(ids(f({ systems: [1], regions: ['usa'], query: 'mario' }))).toEqual([2]);
  });

  it('counts active filters, not the query', () => {
    expect(activeFilterCount(EMPTY_FILTERS)).toBe(0);
    expect(
      activeFilterCount(f({ query: 'x', art: 'complete', systems: [1], sizeMin: 5, modifiedTo: 9 })),
    ).toBe(4);
  });
});

describe('computeFacets', () => {
  it('counts each facet with every other filter applied', () => {
    const view = generateLibrary({ seed: 7, count: 240, root: '/m', generation: 1, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    const keys = buildSearchKeys(view.entries);
    const filters = f({ art: 'missing-cover', systems: [3] });
    const facets = computeFacets(view.entries, keys, filters);
    const inSystem3 = view.entries.filter((x) => x.system === 3);
    // The art facet ignores the art filter but keeps the system filter.
    expect(facets.art['missing-cover']).toBe(inSystem3.filter((x) => x.art.cover === 'missing').length);
    expect(facets.art.all).toBe(inSystem3.length);
    // The systems facet ignores the system filter but keeps the art filter.
    for (const s of view.systems) {
      expect(facets.systems.get(s.index) ?? 0).toBe(
        view.entries.filter((x) => x.system === s.index && x.art.cover === 'missing').length,
      );
    }
    // Facet counts agree with applyFilters.
    expect(facets.systems.get(3) ?? 0).toBe(applyFilters(view.entries, keys, filters).length);
  });
});

describe('date inputs', () => {
  it('turns local dates into inclusive day bounds and back', () => {
    const start = dayStart('2026-10-16');
    const end = dayEnd('2026-10-16');
    expect(start).not.toBeNull();
    expect(end! - start!).toBe(86_399);
    expect(toDateInput(start)).toBe('2026-10-16');
    expect(dayStart('')).toBeNull();
    expect(toDateInput(null)).toBe('');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

import { dateBucket, inScope, sameScope } from './scope';

const DAY = 86_400;
const NOW = 1_000_000_000;
const e = (dir: string, system = 0, mtime = NOW) => ({ dir, system, mtime }) as EntryView;

describe('scopes', () => {
  it('buckets dates by age', () => {
    expect(dateBucket(NOW - DAY, NOW)).toBe('week');
    expect(dateBucket(NOW + DAY, NOW)).toBe('week');
    expect(dateBucket(NOW - 10 * DAY, NOW)).toBe('month');
    expect(dateBucket(NOW - 100 * DAY, NOW)).toBe('year');
    expect(dateBucket(NOW - 400 * DAY, NOW)).toBe('older');
  });

  it('matches folders by path prefix on whole segments', () => {
    const folder = { kind: 'folder', path: 'ROMS/SFC' } as const;
    expect(inScope(e('ROMS/SFC'), folder, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(inScope(e('ROMS/SFC/Hacks'), folder, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(inScope(e('ROMS/SFC2'), folder, NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(inScope(e('anything'), { kind: 'folder', path: '' }, NOW)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches systems, dates and all', () => {
    expect(inScope(e('x', 2), { kind: 'system', system: 2 }, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(inScope(e('x', 1), { kind: 'system', system: 2 }, NOW)).toBe(false);
    expect(inScope(e('x', 0, NOW - 400 * DAY), { kind: 'date', bucket: 'older' }, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(inScope(e('x'), { kind: 'all' }, NOW)).toBe(true);
    expect(sameScope({ kind: 'system', system: 1 }, { kind: 'system', system: 1 })).toBe(true);
    expect(sameScope({ kind: 'system', system: 1 }, { kind: 'all' })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

import { sortEntries } from './sort';

const e = (id: number, name: string, system: number, size: number, mtime: number) =>
  ({ id, name, system, size, mtime }) as EntryView;
const list = [e(0, 'b10', 1, 30, 1), e(1, 'B2', 0, 10, 3), e(2, 'a', 1, 20, 2)];
const names = ['Super Nintendo', 'Game Boy'];
const ids = (xs: EntryView[]) => xs.map((x) => x.id);

describe('sortEntries', () => {
  it('keeps library order by default', () => {
    expect(ids(sortEntries(list, { key: 'library', dir: 'asc' }, names))).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);
  });
  it('sorts names naturally and case-insensitively', () => {
    expect(ids(sortEntries(list, { key: 'name', dir: 'asc' }, names))).toEqual([2, 1, 0]);
  });
  it('sorts by system name, size and date, both directions', () => {

    expect(ids(sortEntries(list, { key: 'size', dir: 'desc' }, names))).toEqual([0, 2, 1]);
    expect(ids(sortEntries(list, { key: 'mtime', dir: 'desc' }, names))).toEqual([1, 2, 0]);
  });
  it('never mutates its input', () => {
    sortEntries(list, { key: 'name', dir: 'asc' }, names);
    expect(ids(list)).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);

});
```

`src/lib/search.ts`:

```ts
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

/** Lowercase without diacritics: `Pokémon` → `pokemon`. */
export function foldText(s: string): string {
  return s.normalize('NFD').replace(/\p{M}/gu, '').toLowerCase();
}

/** Search keys indexed by entry id (`name.ext`, folded). */
export function buildSearchKeys(entries: readonly EntryView[]): string[] {
  const keys: string[] = [];
  for (const e of entries) keys[e.id] = foldText(`${e.name}.${e.ext}`);
  return keys;
}

/** The folded words of a query; every word must appear in the key. */
export function queryTokens(query: string): string[] {
  return foldText(query).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
}
```

`src/lib/filters.ts`:

```ts
import type { EntryView, Region } from '@/ipc/types';

import { queryTokens } from './search';

export type ArtFilter =
  | 'all'
  | 'missing-any'
  | 'missing-cover'
  | 'missing-screenshot'
  | 'missing-title'
  | 'complete';

export const ART_FILTERS: readonly ArtFilter[] = [
  'all',
  'missing-any',
  'missing-cover',
  'missing-screenshot',
  'missing-title',
  'complete',
];

export interface Filters {
  query: string;
  art: ArtFilter;
  /** System indexes; empty = any. */
  systems: readonly number[];
  exts: readonly string[];
  regions: readonly Region[];
  /** Inclusive bounds, Unix seconds. */
  modifiedFrom: number | null;
  modifiedTo: number | null;
  /** Inclusive bounds, bytes. */
  sizeMin: number | null;
  sizeMax: number | null;
}

export const EMPTY_FILTERS: Filters = {
  query: '',
  art: 'all',
  systems: [],
  exts: [],
  regions: [],
  modifiedFrom: null,
  modifiedTo: null,
  sizeMin: null,
  sizeMax: null,
};

export type FacetField = 'art' | 'systems' | 'exts' | 'regions';

/** Any media the layout supports is missing (unsupported never counts). */
export function isMissingAny(e: EntryView): boolean {
  return (
    e.art.cover === 'missing' ||
    e.art.screenshot === 'missing' ||
    e.art.title === 'missing'
  );
}

export function matchesArt(e: EntryView, art: ArtFilter): boolean {
  switch (art) {
    case 'all':
      return true;
    case 'missing-any':
      return isMissingAny(e);
    case 'missing-cover':
      return e.art.cover === 'missing';
    case 'missing-screenshot':
      return e.art.screenshot === 'missing';
    case 'missing-title':
      return e.art.title === 'missing';
    case 'complete':
      return !isMissingAny(e);
  }
}

/** Whether `e` passes every filter except `skip` (for facet counts). */
export function matches(
  e: EntryView,
  key: string,
  f: Filters,
  tokens: readonly string[],
  skip?: FacetField,
): boolean {
  if (tokens.length > 0 && !tokens.every((t) => key.includes(t))) return false;
  if (skip !== 'art' && !matchesArt(e, f.art)) return false;
  if (skip !== 'systems' && f.systems.length > 0 && !f.systems.includes(e.system)) {
    return false;
  }
  if (skip !== 'exts' && f.exts.length > 0 && !f.exts.includes(e.ext)) return false;
  if (
    skip !== 'regions' &&
    f.regions.length > 0 &&
    !e.regions.some((r) => f.regions.includes(r))
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  if (f.modifiedFrom !== null && e.mtime < f.modifiedFrom) return false;
  if (f.modifiedTo !== null && e.mtime > f.modifiedTo) return false;
  if (f.sizeMin !== null && e.size < f.sizeMin) return false;
  if (f.sizeMax !== null && e.size > f.sizeMax) return false;
  return true;
}

export function applyFilters(
  entries: readonly EntryView[],
  keys: readonly string[],
  f: Filters,
): EntryView[] {
  const tokens = queryTokens(f.query);
  return entries.filter((e) => matches(e, keys[e.id] ?? '', f, tokens));
}

export interface Facets {
  art: Record<ArtFilter, number>;
  systems: Map<number, number>;
  exts: Map<string, number>;
  regions: Map<Region, number>;
}

function bump<K>(map: Map<K, number>, key: K): void {
  map.set(key, (map.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
}

/** Per-option counts; each facet ignores its own filter only. */
export function computeFacets(
  entries: readonly EntryView[],
  keys: readonly string[],
  f: Filters,
): Facets {
  const tokens = queryTokens(f.query);
  const art = Object.fromEntries(ART_FILTERS.map((a) => [a, 0])) as Record<
    ArtFilter,
    number
  >;
  const systems = new Map<number, number>();
  const exts = new Map<string, number>();
  const regions = new Map<Region, number>();
  for (const e of entries) {
    const key = keys[e.id] ?? '';
    if (matches(e, key, f, tokens, 'art')) {
      for (const a of ART_FILTERS) if (matchesArt(e, a)) art[a] += 1;
    }
    if (matches(e, key, f, tokens, 'systems')) bump(systems, e.system);
    if (matches(e, key, f, tokens, 'exts')) bump(exts, e.ext);
    if (matches(e, key, f, tokens, 'regions')) {
      for (const r of e.regions) bump(regions, r);
    }
  }
  return { art, systems, exts, regions };
}

/** Filters set besides the search query. */
export function activeFilterCount(f: Filters): number {
  return [
    f.art !== 'all',
    f.systems.length > 0,
    f.exts.length > 0,
    f.regions.length > 0,
    f.modifiedFrom !== null || f.modifiedTo !== null,
    f.sizeMin !== null || f.sizeMax !== null,
  ].filter(Boolean).length;
}

function localSeconds(date: string, time: string): number | null {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return null;
  const ms = new Date(`${date}T${time}`).getTime();
  return Number.isNaN(ms) ? null : Math.floor(ms / 1000);
}

/** `YYYY-MM-DD` (local) → first second of that day. */
export function dayStart(date: string): number | null {
  return localSeconds(date, '00:00:00');
}

/** `YYYY-MM-DD` (local) → last second of that day. */
export function dayEnd(date: string): number | null {
  return localSeconds(date, '23:59:59');
}

/** Unix seconds → `YYYY-MM-DD` in local time (`''` for null). */
export function toDateInput(secs: number | null): string {
  if (secs === null) return '';
  const d = new Date(secs * 1000);
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
}
```

`src/lib/scope.ts`:

```ts
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

export type DateBucket = 'week' | 'month' | 'year' | 'older';
export const DATE_BUCKETS: readonly DateBucket[] = ['week', 'month', 'year', 'older'];

/** What the sidebar shows: one folder subtree, one system, one date bucket, or all. */
export type Scope =
  | { kind: 'all' }
  | { kind: 'system'; system: number }
  | { kind: 'folder'; path: string }
  | { kind: 'date'; bucket: DateBucket };

const DAY = 86_400;

export function dateBucket(mtime: number, now: number): DateBucket {
  const age = now - mtime;
  if (age < 7 * DAY) return 'week';
  if (age < 30 * DAY) return 'month';
  if (age < 365 * DAY) return 'year';
  return 'older';
}

export function inScope(e: EntryView, scope: Scope, now: number): boolean {
  switch (scope.kind) {
    case 'all':
      return true;
    case 'system':
      return e.system === scope.system;
    case 'folder':
      return (
        scope.path === '' ||
        e.dir === scope.path ||
        e.dir.startsWith(`${scope.path}/`)
      );
    case 'date':
      return dateBucket(e.mtime, now) === scope.bucket;
  }
}

export function sameScope(a: Scope, b: Scope): boolean {
  return JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
}
```

`src/lib/sort.ts`:

```ts
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

export type SortKey = 'library' | 'name' | 'system' | 'size' | 'mtime';
export interface Sort {
  key: SortKey;
  dir: 'asc' | 'desc';
}
export const DEFAULT_SORT: Sort = { key: 'library', dir: 'asc' };

const collator = new Intl.Collator(undefined, {
  sensitivity: 'base',
  numeric: true,
});

/** A sorted copy; ties keep library (scan) order. */
export function sortEntries(
  entries: readonly EntryView[],
  sort: Sort,
  systemNames: readonly string[],
): EntryView[] {
  if (sort.key === 'library') {
    return sort.dir === 'asc' ? [...entries] : [...entries].reverse();
  }
  const sign = sort.dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
  const compare = (a: EntryView, b: EntryView): number => {
    switch (sort.key) {
      case 'name':
        return collator.compare(a.name, b.name);
      case 'system':
        return (
          collator.compare(systemNames[a.system] ?? '', systemNames[b.system] ?? '') ||
          collator.compare(a.name, b.name)
        );
      case 'size':
        return a.size - b.size;
      case 'mtime':
      case 'library':
        return a.mtime - b.mtime;
    }
  };
  return [...entries].sort((a, b) => sign * compare(a, b) || a.id - b.id);
}
```

`src/lib/folderTree.ts`:

```ts
export interface FolderNode {
  /** `/`-separated path from the library root (`''` for the root). */
  path: string;
  name: string;
  /** Entries in this folder and below. */
  count: number;
  children: FolderNode[];
}

const collator = new Intl.Collator(undefined, { sensitivity: 'base', numeric: true });

function sortTree(node: FolderNode): void {
  node.children.sort((a, b) => collator.compare(a.name, b.name));
  node.children.forEach(sortTree);
}

/** The folder tree of the entries' `dir`s, with recursive counts. */
export function buildFolderTree(entries: readonly { dir: string }[]): FolderNode {
  const root: FolderNode = { path: '', name: '', count: 0, children: [] };
  const index = new Map<string, FolderNode>([['', root]]);
  for (const e of entries) {
    root.count += 1;
    if (!e.dir) continue;
    let parent = root;
    let path = '';
    for (const part of e.dir.split('/')) {
      path = path ? `${path}/${part}` : part;
      let node = index.get(path);
      if (!node) {
        node = { path, name: part, count: 0, children: [] };
        index.set(path, node);
        parent.children.push(node);
      }
      node.count += 1;
      parent = node;
    }
  }
  sortTree(root);
  return root;
}
```

`src/lib/keyboardNav.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The index to select after `key` in a list/grid of `count` items laid out
 * in `columns` columns with `pageRows` rows per page, or null when the key
 * does not move the selection. `index` is -1 when nothing is selected.
 */
export function nextIndex(
  key: string,
  index: number,
  count: number,
  columns: number,
  pageRows: number,
): number | null {
  if (count === 0) return null;
  const last = count - 1;
  const clamp = (i: number) => Math.min(last, Math.max(0, i));
  if (index < 0) {
    if (key === 'End') return last;
    return ['ArrowRight', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Home', 'PageDown', 'PageUp'].includes(key)
      ? 0
      : null;
  }
  switch (key) {
    case 'ArrowRight':
      return clamp(index + 1);
    case 'ArrowLeft':
      return clamp(index - 1);
    case 'ArrowDown':
      return clamp(index + columns);
    case 'ArrowUp':
      return clamp(index - columns);
    case 'PageDown':
      return clamp(index + columns * pageRows);
    case 'PageUp':
      return clamp(index - columns * pageRows);
    case 'Home':
      return 0;
    case 'End':
      return last;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
```

`src/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
const UNITS = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'] as const;

/** Binary units (1 KB = 1024 B), one decimal, locale separators. */
export function formatSize(bytes: number, locale: string): string {
  let value = bytes;
  let unit = 0;
  while (value >= 1024 && unit < UNITS.length - 1) {
    value /= 1024;
    unit += 1;
  }
  const n = new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
    maximumFractionDigits: unit === 0 ? 0 : 1,
  }).format(value);
  return `${n} ${UNITS[unit]}`;
}

export function formatDate(mtime: number, locale: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { dateStyle: 'medium' }).format(
    new Date(mtime * 1000),
  );
}
```

  - `useLibraryStore`: `{ status: 'idle' | 'opening' | 'ready' | 'error'; view: LibraryView | null; keys: readonly string[]; path: string | null; progress: ScanProgress | null; error: BackendError | null; openingPath: string | null; recents: RecentLibrary[]; loadRecents(): Promise<void>; open(path: string): Promise<void>; forget(path: string): Promise<void>; close(): void }`, `resetLibraryStore()`.
  - `useFilterStore`: `{ filters: Filters; setQuery(q); setArt(a); toggleSystem(i); toggleExt(e); toggleRegion(r); setDates(from, to); setSizes(min, max); clear(); reset() }`.
  - `useViewStore`: `{ scope: Scope; layout: 'grid' | 'list'; sort: Sort; drawerOpen: boolean; filtersOpen: boolean; setScope(s); setLayout(l); toggleSort(k); openDrawer(); closeDrawer(); toggleFilters(); reset() }` (layout persisted in `localStorage['coverly.layout']`).
  - `useSelectionStore`: `{ selectedId: number | null; select(id: number | null) }`.
  - `useVisibleEntries(): { visible: EntryView[]; scoped: EntryView[]; facets: Facets; libraryFacets: Facets; now: number }`.
  - Components: `Root`, `App`, `AppFooter`, `LanguageSwitch`, `ScanProgressView({ path, progress })`, `LibrarySettingsDialog({ onClose })`, `StartScreen`, `LibraryScreen`.

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { setBackend, type Backend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW, generateLibrary } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, MOCK_LIBRARIES } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import type { LibraryView } from '@/ipc/types';

import { useFilterStore } from './filterStore';
import { resetLibraryStore, useLibraryStore } from './libraryStore';
import { useSelectionStore } from './selectionStore';
import { useViewStore } from './viewStore';

const SMALL = MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path;

describe('library store', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW }));
    resetLibraryStore();
  });
  afterEach(() => setBackend(null));

  it('opens a library, indexes it and resets the view', async () => {
    useFilterStore.getState().setQuery('zelda');
    useSelectionStore.getState().select(3);
    useViewStore.getState().setScope({ kind: 'system', system: 2 });
    await useLibraryStore.getState().open(SMALL);
    const s = useLibraryStore.getState();
    expect(s.status).toBe('ready');
    expect(s.path).toBe(SMALL);
    expect(s.view?.entries).toHaveLength(240);
    expect(s.keys).toHaveLength(240);
    expect(s.recents[0]?.path).toBe(SMALL);
    expect(useFilterStore.getState().filters.query).toBe('');
    expect(useSelectionStore.getState().selectedId).toBeNull();
    expect(useViewStore.getState().scope).toEqual({ kind: 'all' });
  });

  it('reports a translated-ready error and stays on Start', async () => {
    await useLibraryStore.getState().open('/mock/Broken');
    const s = useLibraryStore.getState();
    expect(s.status).toBe('error');
    expect(s.error?.code).toBe('not-found');
    expect(s.view).toBeNull();
  });

  it('ignores_a_stale_open_result', async () => {
    const resolvers = new Map<string, (v: LibraryView) => void>();
    const mock = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    const slow: Backend = {
      ...mock,
      openLibrary: (path) =>
        new Promise<LibraryView>((resolve) => resolvers.set(path, resolve)),
    };
    setBackend(slow);
    const first = useLibraryStore.getState().open('/a');
    const second = useLibraryStore.getState().open('/b');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    const view = (name: string, generation: number) =>
      generateLibrary({ seed: 1, count: 3, root: `/${name}`, generation, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    resolvers.get('/b')!(view('b', 2));
    resolvers.get('/a')!(view('a', 1));
    await Promise.all([first, second]);
    expect(useLibraryStore.getState().view?.name).toBe('b');
    expect(useLibraryStore.getState().path).toBe('/b');
  });

  it('closes back to idle', async () => {
    await useLibraryStore.getState().open(SMALL);
    useLibraryStore.getState().close();
    expect(useLibraryStore.getState().status).toBe('idle');
    expect(useLibraryStore.getState().view).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/stores/localeStore.ts`:

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';

import type { Locale } from '@/i18n/core';

export type LocaleChoice = 'auto' | Locale;
const KEY = 'coverly.locale';

function readChoice(): LocaleChoice {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    return v === 'en' || v === 'es' ? v : 'auto';
  } catch {
    return 'auto';
  }
}

interface LocaleState {
  choice: LocaleChoice;
  setChoice(choice: LocaleChoice): void;
}

/** UI language until M5 settings exist (a per-viewer convenience). */
export const useLocaleStore = create<LocaleState>()((set) => ({
  choice: readChoice(),
  setChoice(choice) {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(KEY, choice);
    } catch {
      // Storage may be blocked; the choice still applies to this session.
    }
    set({ choice });
  },
}));
```

`src/stores/filterStore.ts`:

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';

import type { Region } from '@/ipc/types';
import { EMPTY_FILTERS, type ArtFilter, type Filters } from '@/lib/filters';

function toggle<T>(list: readonly T[], value: T): T[] {
  return list.includes(value) ? list.filter((x) => x !== value) : [...list, value];
}

interface FilterState {
  filters: Filters;
  setQuery(query: string): void;
  setArt(art: ArtFilter): void;
  toggleSystem(index: number): void;
  toggleExt(ext: string): void;
  toggleRegion(region: Region): void;
  setDates(from: number | null, to: number | null): void;
  setSizes(min: number | null, max: number | null): void;
  /** Clears every filter but keeps the search text. */
  clear(): void;
  /** Back to no filters and no search. */
  reset(): void;
}

export const useFilterStore = create<FilterState>()((set) => {
  const patch = (p: Partial<Filters>) =>
    set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, ...p } }));
  return {
    filters: EMPTY_FILTERS,
    setQuery: (query) => patch({ query }),
    setArt: (art) => patch({ art }),
    toggleSystem: (i) =>
      set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, systems: toggle(s.filters.systems, i) } })),
    toggleExt: (e) =>
      set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, exts: toggle(s.filters.exts, e) } })),
    toggleRegion: (r) =>
      set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, regions: toggle(s.filters.regions, r) } })),
    setDates: (modifiedFrom, modifiedTo) => patch({ modifiedFrom, modifiedTo }),
    setSizes: (sizeMin, sizeMax) => patch({ sizeMin, sizeMax }),
    clear: () => set((s) => ({ filters: { ...EMPTY_FILTERS, query: s.filters.query } })),
    reset: () => set({ filters: EMPTY_FILTERS }),
  };
});
```

`src/stores/viewStore.ts`:

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';

import type { Scope } from '@/lib/scope';
import { DEFAULT_SORT, type Sort, type SortKey } from '@/lib/sort';

export type Layout = 'grid' | 'list';
const LAYOUT_KEY = 'coverly.layout';

function readLayout(): Layout {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(LAYOUT_KEY) === 'list' ? 'list' : 'grid';
  } catch {
    return 'grid';
  }
}

interface ViewState {
  scope: Scope;
  layout: Layout;
  sort: Sort;
  drawerOpen: boolean;
  filtersOpen: boolean;
  setScope(scope: Scope): void;
  setLayout(layout: Layout): void;
  /** Same key flips the direction; another key sorts ascending. */
  toggleSort(key: SortKey): void;
  openDrawer(): void;
  closeDrawer(): void;
  toggleFilters(): void;
  /** Per-library state back to defaults (the layout is kept). */
  reset(): void;
}

export const useViewStore = create<ViewState>()((set) => ({
  scope: { kind: 'all' },
  layout: readLayout(),
  sort: DEFAULT_SORT,
  drawerOpen: false,
  filtersOpen: false,
  setScope: (scope) => set({ scope }),
  setLayout(layout) {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(LAYOUT_KEY, layout);
    } catch {
      // Remembering the layout is a convenience only.
    }
    set({ layout });
  },
  toggleSort: (key) =>
    set((s) => ({
      sort:
        s.sort.key === key
          ? { key, dir: s.sort.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' }
          : { key, dir: 'asc' },
    })),
  openDrawer: () => set({ drawerOpen: true }),
  closeDrawer: () => set({ drawerOpen: false }),
  toggleFilters: () => set((s) => ({ filtersOpen: !s.filtersOpen })),
  reset: () =>
    set({ scope: { kind: 'all' }, sort: DEFAULT_SORT, drawerOpen: false, filtersOpen: false }),
}));
```

`src/stores/libraryStore.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The opened library and the Start screen state. `open()` is safe against
 * double opens: only the latest request may change the state.
 */
import { create } from 'zustand';

import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import type { LibraryView, RecentLibrary, ScanProgress } from '@/ipc/types';
import { buildSearchKeys } from '@/lib/search';

import { useFilterStore } from './filterStore';
import { useSelectionStore } from './selectionStore';
import { useViewStore } from './viewStore';

export type LibraryStatus = 'idle' | 'opening' | 'ready' | 'error';

interface LibraryState {
  status: LibraryStatus;
  view: LibraryView | null;
  /** Search keys indexed by entry id. */
  keys: readonly string[];
  /** Folder of the open library, as picked. */
  path: string | null;
  progress: ScanProgress | null;
  error: BackendError | null;
  openingPath: string | null;
  recents: RecentLibrary[];
  loadRecents(): Promise<void>;
  open(path: string): Promise<void>;
  forget(path: string): Promise<void>;
  close(): void;
}

const initial = {
  status: 'idle' as LibraryStatus,
  view: null,
  keys: [],
  path: null,
  progress: null,
  error: null,
  openingPath: null,
  recents: [],
};

let requestSeq = 0;

export const useLibraryStore = create<LibraryState>()((set, get) => ({
  ...initial,
  async loadRecents() {
    try {
      const backend = await getBackend();
      set({ recents: (await backend.recentLibraries()) ?? [] });
    } catch {
      set({ recents: [] });
    }
  },
  async open(path) {
    const seq = (requestSeq += 1);
    set({ status: 'opening', openingPath: path, progress: null, error: null });
    const backend = await getBackend();
    const unlisten = await backend.onScanProgress((progress) => {
      if (seq === requestSeq) set({ progress });
    });
    try {
      const view = await backend.openLibrary(path);
      if (seq !== requestSeq) return;
      useFilterStore.getState().reset();
      useViewStore.getState().reset();
      useSelectionStore.getState().select(null);
      set({
        status: 'ready',
        view,
        keys: buildSearchKeys(view.entries),
        path,
        progress: null,
        openingPath: null,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (seq !== requestSeq) return;
      const error = toBackendError(e);
      if (error.code === 'superseded') return;
      set({ status: 'error', error, progress: null, openingPath: null });
    } finally {
      unlisten();
      if (seq === requestSeq) void get().loadRecents();
    }
  },
  async forget(path) {
    try {
      const backend = await getBackend();
      set({ recents: await backend.forgetRecentLibrary(path) });
    } catch (e) {
      set({ error: toBackendError(e) });
    }
  },
  close() {
    requestSeq += 1;
    useSelectionStore.getState().select(null);
    useViewStore.getState().closeDrawer();
    set({ ...initial, recents: get().recents });
  },
}));

/** Test helper: back to the initial state. */
export function resetLibraryStore(): void {
  requestSeq += 1;
  useLibraryStore.setState(initial);
  useFilterStore.getState().reset();
  useViewStore.getState().reset();
  useSelectionStore.getState().select(null);
}
```

`src/stores/useVisibleEntries.ts`:

```ts
import { useDeferredValue, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { applyFilters, computeFacets, type Facets } from '@/lib/filters';
import { inScope } from '@/lib/scope';
import { sortEntries } from '@/lib/sort';

import { useFilterStore } from './filterStore';
import { useLibraryStore } from './libraryStore';
import { useViewStore } from './viewStore';

export interface VisibleEntries {
  /** In scope, filtered and sorted: what the grid/table shows. */
  visible: EntryView[];
  /** In scope, before filters. */
  scoped: EntryView[];
  /** Facet counts within the scope (filter panel). */
  facets: Facets;
  /** Facet counts over the whole library (sidebar). */
  libraryFacets: Facets;
  /** Unix seconds the date buckets are relative to. */
  now: number;
}

const NO_ENTRIES: EntryView[] = [];

export function useVisibleEntries(): VisibleEntries {
  const view = useLibraryStore((s) => s.view);
  const keys = useLibraryStore((s) => s.keys);
  const filters = useDeferredValue(useFilterStore((s) => s.filters));
  const scope = useViewStore((s) => s.scope);
  const sort = useViewStore((s) => s.sort);
  const [now] = useState(() => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
  const entries = view?.entries ?? NO_ENTRIES;

  const scoped = useMemo(
    () => entries.filter((e) => inScope(e, scope, now)),
    [entries, scope, now],
  );
  const systemNames = useMemo(() => view?.systems.map((s) => s.name) ?? [], [view]);
  const visible = useMemo(
    () => sortEntries(applyFilters(scoped, keys, filters), sort, systemNames),
    [scoped, keys, filters, sort, systemNames],
  );
  const facets = useMemo(() => computeFacets(scoped, keys, filters), [scoped, keys, filters]);
  const libraryFacets = useMemo(
    () => computeFacets(entries, keys, filters),
    [entries, keys, filters],
  );
  return { visible, scoped, facets, libraryFacets, now };
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import App from '@/App';
import { I18nProvider } from '@/i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, type MockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { Root } from '@/Root';
import { useLocaleStore } from '@/stores/localeStore';
import { resetLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';

let backend: MockBackend;

function renderApp(locale: 'en' | 'es' = 'en') {
  return render(
    <I18nProvider locale={locale}>
      <App />
    </I18nProvider>,
  );
}

describe('Start screen', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    backend = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    setBackend(backend);
    resetLibraryStore();
  });
  afterEach(() => setBackend(null));

  it('lists recent libraries with their ROM counts', async () => {
    renderApp();
    const small = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^Small library/ });
    expect(within(small).getByText('240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('ejected_recent_is_disabled', async () => {
    renderApp();
    const ejected = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^EJECTED/ });
    expect(ejected).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(ejected).getByText('Not connected')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('opens a recent library', async () => {
    renderApp();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^Small library/ }));
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Small library' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('opens the folder picked in the dialog', async () => {
    renderApp();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open folder…' }));
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Fixture SD (12k)' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('opens a dropped folder', async () => {
    renderApp();
    await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^Small library/ });
    const drop = new Event('drop', { cancelable: true });
    Object.defineProperty(drop, 'dataTransfer', {
      value: { files: [], getData: () => 'Small library' },
    });
    window.dispatchEvent(drop);
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Small library' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows_a_translated_error_when_opening_fails', async () => {
    backend.pickFolder = () => Promise.resolve('/mock/Broken');
    renderApp('es');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Abrir carpeta…' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Esta carpeta no existe: /mock/Broken',
    );
  });

  it('forgets a recent library', async () => {
    renderApp();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Forget Empty library' }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /^Empty library/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument(),
    );
  });

  it('edits the ignored folders', async () => {
    renderApp();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Library settings' }));
    const box = await screen.findByLabelText('Ignored folders');
    await waitFor(() => expect(box).toHaveValue('bios\nsaves\nstates\nShortcuts'));
    await userEvent.clear(box);
    await userEvent.type(box, 'bios{enter}Saves{enter}bios');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect((await backend.librarySettings()).ignored_dirs).toEqual(['bios', 'Saves']);
  });

  it('switches the language', async () => {
    render(<Root />);
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Language'), 'es');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Abrir carpeta…' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    useLocaleStore.getState().setChoice('auto');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { clearMocks, mockIPC } from '@tauri-apps/api/mocks';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import App from './App';
import { I18nProvider } from './i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from './ipc/backend';
import { createMockBackend } from './ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { resetLibraryStore } from './stores/libraryStore';

function renderApp(locale: 'en' | 'es' = 'en') {
  return render(
    <I18nProvider locale={locale}>
      <App />
    </I18nProvider>,
  );
}

describe('App start screen', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0 }));
    resetLibraryStore();
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    setBackend(null);
    clearMocks();
  });

  it('shows the wordmark and the tagline', () => {
    renderApp();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Coverly ROMs' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Find and add the missing cover art in your ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the tagline in Spanish', () => {
    renderApp('es');
    expect(
      screen.getByText('Encuentra y añade las portadas que faltan en tus ROMs'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('has an enabled Open folder button', () => {
    renderApp();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open folder…' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('renders a dash for versions outside Tauri', () => {
    renderApp();
    expect(screen.getByText('Version —')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Core —')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the versions reported by app_info inside Tauri', async () => {
    mockIPC((cmd) =>
      cmd === 'app_info'
        ? { name: 'Coverly ROMs', version: '9.9.9', core_version: '1.2.3' }
        : undefined,
    );
    renderApp();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Version 9.9.9')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Core 1.2.3')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import App from './App';
import { I18nProvider } from './i18n/I18nProvider';
import { useLocaleStore } from './stores/localeStore';

/** The app inside the i18n provider, following the language switch. */
export function Root() {
  const choice = useLocaleStore((s) => s.choice);
  return (
    <I18nProvider {...(choice === 'auto' ? {} : { locale: choice })}>
      <App />
    </I18nProvider>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { fetchAppInfo, type AppInfo } from '@/ipc/appInfo';

const NO_VALUE = '—';

export function AppFooter() {
  const t = useT();
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<AppInfo | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    void fetchAppInfo().then((result) => {
      if (!cancelled) setInfo(result);
    });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <footer className="footer flex justify-center gap-4 px-6 py-3">
      <span>{t('footer.version', { version: info?.version ?? NO_VALUE })}</span>
      <span>{t('footer.coreVersion', { version: info?.core_version ?? NO_VALUE })}</span>
    </footer>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { useLocaleStore, type LocaleChoice } from '@/stores/localeStore';

const CHOICES: readonly LocaleChoice[] = ['auto', 'en', 'es'];

export function LanguageSwitch() {
  const t = useT();
  const choice = useLocaleStore((s) => s.choice);
  const setChoice = useLocaleStore((s) => s.setChoice);
  return (
    <select
      aria-label={t('language.label')}
      className="select"
      value={choice}
      onChange={(e) => setChoice(e.target.value as LocaleChoice)}
    >
      {CHOICES.map((c) => (
        <option key={c} value={c}>
          {t(`language.${c}`)}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { ScanProgress } from '@/ipc/types';

export function ScanProgressView({
  path,
  progress,
}: {
  path: string | null;
  progress: ScanProgress | null;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  return (
    <div role="status" className="scan-progress">
      <p>
        {progress && progress.done > 0
          ? t('start.scanning', {
              folder: progress.folder,
              done: progress.done,
              total: progress.total,
            })
          : t('start.scanningStart')}
      </p>
      <progress
        aria-label={t('start.progressLabel')}
        max={progress?.total || 1}
        value={progress?.done ?? 0}
      />
      {progress && <p className="hint">{t('start.romsFound', { n: progress.roms })}</p>}
      {path && <p className="hint truncate" title={path}>{path}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```
