`src/components/LibrarySettingsDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { errorKey, toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';

/** Edits the ignored folders; saving rescans the open library. */
export function LibrarySettingsDialog({ onClose }: { onClose(): void }) {
  const t = useT();
  const [text, setText] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<BackendError | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void getBackend()
      .then((b) => b.librarySettings())
      .then(
        (s) => alive && setText(s.ignored_dirs.join('\n')),
        (e: unknown) => alive && setError(toBackendError(e)),
      );
    const onKey = (ev: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (ev.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  async function save() {
    try {
      const backend = await getBackend();
      await backend.setLibrarySettings({ ignored_dirs: (text ?? '').split('\n') });
      onClose();
      const { status, path, open } = useLibraryStore.getState();
      if (status === 'ready' && path) await open(path);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(toBackendError(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="modal-backdrop">
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="library-settings-title" className="modal">
        <h2 id="library-settings-title" className="section-title">
          {t('settings.title')}
        </h2>
        <label htmlFor="ignored-dirs" className="field-label">
          {t('settings.ignoredDirs')}
        </label>
        <textarea
          id="ignored-dirs"
          className="textarea"
          rows={6}
          value={text ?? ''}
          disabled={text === null}
          aria-describedby="ignored-dirs-hint"
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        />
        <p id="ignored-dirs-hint" className="hint">
          {t('settings.ignoredDirsHint')}
        </p>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="error-banner">
            {t(errorKey(error.code), { detail: error.detail ?? '' })}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="modal-actions">
          <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('settings.cancel')}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="primary-button"
            disabled={text === null}
            onClick={() => void save()}
          >
            {t('settings.save')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/screens/StartScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { AppFooter } from '@/components/AppFooter';
import { LanguageSwitch } from '@/components/LanguageSwitch';
import { LibrarySettingsDialog } from '@/components/LibrarySettingsDialog';
import { ScanProgressView } from '@/components/ScanProgressView';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { errorKey } from '@/ipc/errors';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';

export function StartScreen() {
  const t = useT();
  const status = useLibraryStore((s) => s.status);
  const recents = useLibraryStore((s) => s.recents);
  const progress = useLibraryStore((s) => s.progress);
  const error = useLibraryStore((s) => s.error);
  const openingPath = useLibraryStore((s) => s.openingPath);
  const loadRecents = useLibraryStore((s) => s.loadRecents);
  const open = useLibraryStore((s) => s.open);
  const forget = useLibraryStore((s) => s.forget);
  const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const closeSettings = useCallback(() => setSettingsOpen(false), []);
  const opening = status === 'opening';

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadRecents();
  }, [loadRecents]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    let off: (() => void) | undefined;
    void getBackend()
      .then((b) => b.onFolderDrop((path) => void open(path)))
      .then(
        (unlisten) => {
          if (alive) off = unlisten;
          else unlisten();
        },
        () => undefined,
      );
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      off?.();
    };
  }, [open]);

  async function pick() {
    const backend = await getBackend();
    const path = await backend.pickFolder();
    if (path) await open(path);
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <header className="app-header flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-6 py-3">
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(true)}>
          {t('start.settings')}
        </button>
        <LanguageSwitch />
      </header>
      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-6 px-6 py-10 text-center">
        <h1 className="wordmark">{t('app.name')}</h1>
        <p className="tagline max-w-xl">{t('app.tagline')}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="primary-button"
          disabled={opening}
          onClick={() => void pick()}
        >
          {t('start.openFolder')}
        </button>
        <p className="hint">{t('start.dropHint')}</p>
        {opening && <ScanProgressView path={openingPath} progress={progress} />}
        {status === 'error' && error && (
          <p role="alert" className="error-banner">
            {t(errorKey(error.code), { detail: error.detail ?? '' })}
          </p>
        )}
        <section aria-labelledby="recent-heading" className="recent w-full max-w-2xl text-left">
          <h2 id="recent-heading" className="section-title">
            {t('start.recent')}
          </h2>
          {recents.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="hint">{t('start.noRecent')}</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="recent-list">
              {recents.map((r) => (
                <li key={r.path} className="recent-item">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="recent-open"
                    disabled={!r.available || opening}
                    onClick={() => void open(r.path)}
                  >
                    <span className="recent-name truncate" title={r.name}>
                      {r.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="recent-path truncate" title={r.path}>
                      {r.path}
                    </span>
                    <span className="recent-meta">
                      {r.available ? t('start.roms', { n: r.roms }) : t('start.notConnected')}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="icon-button"
                    aria-label={t('start.forget', { name: r.name })}
                    onClick={() => void forget(r.path)}
                  >
                    ×
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>
      <AppFooter />
      {settingsOpen && <LibrarySettingsDialog onClose={closeSettings} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';

export function LibraryScreen() {
  const t = useT();
  const view = useLibraryStore((s) => s.view);
  const close = useLibraryStore((s) => s.close);
  if (!view) return null;
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <header className="toolbar">
        <h1 className="toolbar-name truncate" title={view.root}>
          {view.name}
        </h1>
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={close}>
          {t('library.close')}
        </button>
      </header>
      <main className="flex-1 p-6">
        <p>{t('library.count', { n: view.entries.length })}</p>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
// jsdom lacks these browser APIs used by the explorer.
class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe(): void {}
  unobserve(): void {}
  disconnect(): void {}
}
if (typeof globalThis.ResizeObserver === 'undefined') {
  (globalThis as { ResizeObserver?: unknown }).ResizeObserver = ResizeObserverStub;
}
if (typeof URL.createObjectURL !== 'function') {
  URL.createObjectURL = () => 'blob:stub';
}
if (typeof URL.revokeObjectURL !== 'function') {
  URL.revokeObjectURL = () => undefined;
}
if (typeof Element.prototype.scrollTo !== 'function') {
  Element.prototype.scrollTo = () => undefined;
}
```

`src/test/layout.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest';

type SizeProp = 'offsetWidth' | 'offsetHeight' | 'clientWidth' | 'clientHeight';

/** Gives every element a `width`×`height` layout box; returns the undo. */
export function stubLayout(width: number, height: number): () => void {
  const props: [SizeProp, number][] = [
    ['offsetWidth', width],
    ['offsetHeight', height],
    ['clientWidth', width],
    ['clientHeight', height],
  ];
  const saved = props.map(
    ([p]) => [p, Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLElement.prototype, p)] as const,
  );
  for (const [p, value] of props) {
    Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype, p, {
      configurable: true,
      get: () => value,
    });
  }
  const rect = vi.spyOn(HTMLElement.prototype, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue({
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: width,
    bottom: height,
    width,
    height,
    toJSON: () => ({}),
  } as DOMRect);
  return () => {
    rect.mockRestore();
    for (const [p, descriptor] of saved) {
      if (descriptor) Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype, p, descriptor);
      else Reflect.deleteProperty(HTMLElement.prototype, p);
    }
  };
}
```

`src/lib/artCache.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { createMockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';

import { ART_CACHE_SIZE, artKey, clearArtCache, loadArtUrl, peekArtUrl } from './artCache';

describe('art cache', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    clearArtCache();
    setBackend(null);
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('dedupes concurrent loads and revokes evicted URLs', async () => {
    const readArt = vi.fn(async () => ({
      bytes: new Uint8Array([1]),
      mime: 'image/png',
    }));
    setBackend({ ...createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0 }), readArt });
    let n = 0;
    vi.spyOn(URL, 'createObjectURL').mockImplementation(() => `blob:${(n += 1)}`);
    const revoke = vi.spyOn(URL, 'revokeObjectURL').mockImplementation(() => undefined);

    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([loadArtUrl(1, 0, 'cover'), loadArtUrl(1, 0, 'cover')]);
    expect(a).toBe(b);
    expect(readArt).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(peekArtUrl(artKey(1, 0, 'cover'))).toBe(a);

    for (let id = 1; id <= ART_CACHE_SIZE; id += 1) await loadArtUrl(1, id, 'cover');
    expect(revoke).toHaveBeenCalledWith(a);
    expect(peekArtUrl(artKey(1, 0, 'cover'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('does not cache failures', async () => {
    const readArt = vi.fn(async () => {
      throw new Error('nope');
    });
    setBackend({ ...createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0 }), readArt });
    await expect(loadArtUrl(1, 0, 'cover')).rejects.toThrow('nope');
    await expect(loadArtUrl(1, 0, 'cover')).rejects.toThrow('nope');
    expect(readArt).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { I18nProvider } from '@/i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW, generateLibrary, HOSTILE_NAMES } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, MOCK_LIBRARIES } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { clearArtCache } from '@/lib/artCache';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';

import { CoverGrid } from './CoverGrid';

/** The performance budget: tiles mounted at once. */
const MAX_TILES = 120;

const big = generateLibrary({ seed: 12, count: 12_000, root: '/mock/Big', generation: 1, now: FIXTURE_NOW });

function Harness({ entries }: { entries: readonly EntryView[] }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [opened, setOpened] = useState<number | null>(null);
  return (
    <I18nProvider locale="en">
      <CoverGrid
        entries={entries}
        systems={big.systems}
        generation={1}
        selectedId={selected}
        onSelect={setSelected}
        onOpen={(id) => {
          setSelected(id);
          setOpened(id);
        }}
      />
      <output data-testid="opened">{opened ?? ''}</output>
    </I18nProvider>
  );
}

describe('CoverGrid', () => {
  let restore: () => void;
  beforeEach(() => {
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
    setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW }));
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    clearArtCache();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('mounts only the visible tiles of a 12k library', () => {
    render(<Harness entries={big.entries} />);
    const cells = screen.getAllByRole('gridcell');
    expect(cells.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(cells.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_TILES);
  });

  it('moves the selection with the keyboard and opens with Enter', () => {
    render(<Harness entries={big.entries} />);
    const grid = screen.getByRole('grid');
    const columns = Number(grid.getAttribute('aria-colcount'));
    expect(columns).toBe(7);
    grid.focus();
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'ArrowRight' });
    expect(grid).toHaveAttribute('aria-activedescendant', `entry-${big.entries[0]!.id}`);
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'ArrowRight' });
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'ArrowDown' });
    expect(grid).toHaveAttribute('aria-activedescendant', `entry-${big.entries[1 + columns]!.id}`);
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'End' });
    const last = big.entries.at(-1)!.id;
    expect(grid).toHaveAttribute('aria-activedescendant', `entry-${last}`);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('gridcell').length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_TILES);
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'Enter' });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('opened')).toHaveTextContent(String(last));
  });

  it('opens a tile on click', () => {
    render(<Harness entries={big.entries} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByRole('gridcell')[2]!);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('opened')).toHaveTextContent(String(big.entries[2]!.id));
  });

  it('loads cover art lazily for present covers only', async () => {
    // Open the same fixture in the mock so generation 1 serves art.
    const backend = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    setBackend(backend);
    expect((await backend.openLibrary(MOCK_LIBRARIES[0].path)).generation).toBe(1);
    const entries = big.entries.slice(0, 14);
    const { container } = render(<Harness entries={entries} />);
    const present = entries.filter((e) => e.art.cover === 'present').length;
    await waitFor(() => expect(container.querySelectorAll('img')).toHaveLength(present));
  });

  it('renders_hostile_names_as_text', () => {
    const small = generateLibrary({ seed: 7, count: 240, root: '/m', generation: 1, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
    const hostile = small.entries.filter((e) =>
      (HOSTILE_NAMES as readonly string[]).includes(e.name),
    );
    const { container } = render(<Harness entries={hostile} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(container.querySelector('img[src="x"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(container.querySelector('[onerror]')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/hooks/useElementSize.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';

export interface Size {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** A callback ref, the element's size (kept current) and the element. */
export function useElementSize<T extends HTMLElement>(): [
  (el: T | null) => void,
  Size,
  RefObject<T | null>,
] {
  const [size, setSize] = useState<Size>({ width: 0, height: 0 });
  const element = useRef<T | null>(null);
  const observer = useRef<ResizeObserver | null>(null);
  const ref = useCallback((el: T | null) => {
    observer.current?.disconnect();
    observer.current = null;
    element.current = el;
    if (!el) return;
    const measure = () => {
      const next = {
        width: el.clientWidth || el.offsetWidth,
        height: el.clientHeight || el.offsetHeight,
      };
      setSize((prev) =>
        prev.width === next.width && prev.height === next.height ? prev : next,
      );
    };
    measure();
    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
      observer.current = new ResizeObserver(measure);
      observer.current.observe(el);
    }
  }, []);
  return [ref, size, element];
}
```

`src/lib/artCache.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Object URLs of art images, shared by the grid and the drawer: at most
 * ART_CACHE_SIZE live URLs (least recently used are revoked), one request
 * per image at a time, failures never cached.
 */
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import type { Media } from '@/ipc/types';

export const ART_CACHE_SIZE = 400;

const urls = new Map<string, string>();
const inflight = new Map<string, Promise<string>>();

export function artKey(generation: number, id: number, media: Media): string {
  return `${generation}:${id}:${media}`;
}

/** The cached URL (marking it recently used), or null. */
export function peekArtUrl(key: string): string | null {
  const url = urls.get(key);
  if (url === undefined) return null;
  urls.delete(key);
  urls.set(key, url);
  return url;
}

function evict(): void {
  while (urls.size > ART_CACHE_SIZE) {
    const oldest = urls.keys().next().value;
    if (oldest === undefined) return;
    const url = urls.get(oldest);
    urls.delete(oldest);
    if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }
}

export function loadArtUrl(generation: number, id: number, media: Media): Promise<string> {
  const key = artKey(generation, id, media);
  const hit = peekArtUrl(key);
  if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit);
  const pending = inflight.get(key);
  if (pending) return pending;
  const request = (async () => {
    const backend = await getBackend();
    const image = await backend.readArt(generation, id, media);
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([image.bytes], { type: image.mime }));
    urls.set(key, url);
    evict();
    return url;
  })().finally(() => inflight.delete(key));
  inflight.set(key, request);
  return request;
}

export function clearArtCache(): void {
  for (const url of urls.values()) URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  urls.clear();
  inflight.clear();
}
```

`src/hooks/useArtUrl.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import type { Media } from '@/ipc/types';
import { artKey, loadArtUrl, peekArtUrl } from '@/lib/artCache';

/** The object URL of one art image once loaded; null while loading, missing or failed. */
export function useArtUrl(
  generation: number,
  id: number,
  media: Media,
  enabled: boolean,
): string | null {
  const key = artKey(generation, id, media);
  const cached = enabled ? peekArtUrl(key) : null;
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<{ key: string; url: string | null } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || cached) return;
    let alive = true;
    loadArtUrl(generation, id, media).then(
      (url) => alive && setLoaded({ key, url }),
      () => alive && setLoaded({ key, url: null }),
    );
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [enabled, cached, key, generation, id, media]);
  if (!enabled) return null;
  return cached ?? (loaded?.key === key ? loaded.url : null);
}
```

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { ArtMark, Media } from '@/ipc/types';

const GLYPH: Record<ArtMark, string> = { present: '●', missing: '○', unsupported: '–' };

export function ArtStatusIcon({ media, mark }: { media: Media; mark: ArtMark }) {
  const t = useT();
  const label = t('art.status', {
    media: t(`art.media.${media}`),
    state: t(`art.mark.${mark}`),
  });
  return (
    <span role="img" aria-label={label} title={label} className={`art-mark art-mark--${mark}`}>
      {GLYPH[mark]}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/ArtThumb.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useArtUrl } from '@/hooks/useArtUrl';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { EntryView, Media } from '@/ipc/types';

export function ArtThumb({
  generation,
  entry,
  media,
  className = '',
}: {
  generation: number;
  entry: EntryView;
  media: Media;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const mark = entry.art[media];
  const url = useArtUrl(generation, entry.id, media, mark === 'present');
  if (url) {
    return <img src={url} alt="" draggable={false} className={`art-thumb ${className}`} />;
  }
  return (
    <div className={`art-thumb art-placeholder ${className}`} aria-hidden="true">
      {mark === 'present'
        ? ''
        : t(mark === 'missing' ? `art.none.${media}` : 'art.mark.unsupported')}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/CoverGrid.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual';
import { useMemo, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent } from 'react';

import { useElementSize } from '@/hooks/useElementSize';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { EntryView, SystemView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { nextIndex } from '@/lib/keyboardNav';

import { ArtStatusIcon } from './ArtStatusIcon';
import { ArtThumb } from './ArtThumb';

export const TILE_W = 168;
export const TILE_H = 264;
export const GAP = 12;

export interface EntryListProps {
  entries: readonly EntryView[];
  systems: readonly SystemView[];
  generation: number;
  selectedId: number | null;
  /** The keyboard moved the selection. */
  onSelect(id: number): void;
  /** Click or Enter: select and show details. */
  onOpen(id: number): void;
}

/** The entry id of the item an event happened in, if any. */
export function entryIdOf(target: EventTarget | null): number | null {
  const el = target instanceof Element ? target.closest('[data-entry-id]') : null;
  const raw = el?.getAttribute('data-entry-id');
  return raw == null ? null : Number(raw);
}

export function CoverGrid({
  entries,
  systems,
  generation,
  selectedId,
  onSelect,
  onOpen,
}: EntryListProps) {
  const t = useT();
  const [ref, size, element] = useElementSize<HTMLDivElement>();
  const columns = Math.max(1, Math.floor((size.width - GAP) / (TILE_W + GAP)));
  const rows = Math.ceil(entries.length / columns);
  const pageRows = Math.max(1, Math.floor(size.height / (TILE_H + GAP)));
  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: rows,
    getScrollElement: () => element.current,
    estimateSize: () => TILE_H + GAP,
    overscan: 2,
    initialRect: size,
  });
  const indexOf = useMemo(() => new Map(entries.map((e, i) => [e.id, i])), [entries]);
  const selectedIndex = selectedId === null ? -1 : (indexOf.get(selectedId) ?? -1);

  function onKeyDown(ev: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (ev.key === 'Enter') {
      if (selectedId !== null) {
        ev.preventDefault();
        onOpen(selectedId);
      }
      return;
    }
    const next = nextIndex(ev.key, selectedIndex, entries.length, columns, pageRows);
    const entry = next === null ? undefined : entries[next];
    if (next === null || !entry) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    onSelect(entry.id);
    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(Math.floor(next / columns), { align: 'auto' });
  }

  function onClick(ev: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const id = entryIdOf(ev.target);
    if (id !== null) onOpen(id);
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      role="grid"
      aria-label={t('library.grid.label')}
      aria-rowcount={rows}
      aria-colcount={columns}
      aria-activedescendant={selectedId === null ? undefined : `entry-${selectedId}`}
      tabIndex={0}
      className="cover-grid"
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }}>
        {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map((row) => (
          <div
            key={row.key}
            role="row"
            aria-rowindex={row.index + 1}
            className="cover-row"
            style={{ transform: `translateY(${row.start}px)`, height: TILE_H, gap: GAP, paddingLeft: GAP }}
          >
            {entries.slice(row.index * columns, (row.index + 1) * columns).map((entry) => (
              <div
                key={entry.id}
                id={`entry-${entry.id}`}
                role="gridcell"
                aria-selected={entry.id === selectedId}
                data-entry-id={entry.id}
                className="cover-tile"
                style={{ width: TILE_W }}
                title={entry.name}
              >
                <ArtThumb generation={generation} entry={entry} media="cover" />
                <span className="cover-tile__name">{entry.name}</span>
                <span className="cover-tile__meta">
                  {systems[entry.system]?.folder ?? ''} · {entry.ext}
                </span>
                <span className="cover-tile__marks">
                  <ArtStatusIcon media="cover" mark={entry.art.cover} />
                  <ArtStatusIcon media="screenshot" mark={entry.art.screenshot} />
                  <ArtStatusIcon media="title" mark={entry.art.title} />
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/EntryTable.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual';
import { useContext, useMemo, type KeyboardEvent, type MouseEvent } from 'react';

import { useElementSize } from '@/hooks/useElementSize';
import { LocaleContext } from '@/i18n/core';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { MEDIA } from '@/ipc/types';
import { formatDate, formatSize } from '@/lib/format';
import { nextIndex } from '@/lib/keyboardNav';
import type { Sort, SortKey } from '@/lib/sort';

import { ArtStatusIcon } from './ArtStatusIcon';
import { entryIdOf, type EntryListProps } from './CoverGrid';

export const ROW_H = 34;

const COLUMNS: readonly { key: SortKey; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'name', label: 'columns.name' },
  { key: 'system', label: 'columns.system' },
  { key: 'size', label: 'columns.size' },
  { key: 'mtime', label: 'columns.modified' },
];

export interface EntryTableProps extends EntryListProps {
  sort: Sort;
  onSort(key: SortKey): void;
}

export function EntryTable({
  entries,
  systems,
  selectedId,
  onSelect,
  onOpen,
  sort,
  onSort,
}: EntryTableProps) {
  const t = useT();
  const locale = useContext(LocaleContext);
  const [ref, size, element] = useElementSize<HTMLDivElement>();
  const pageRows = Math.max(1, Math.floor(size.height / ROW_H) - 1);
  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: entries.length,
    getScrollElement: () => element.current,
    estimateSize: () => ROW_H,
    overscan: 8,
    scrollPaddingStart: ROW_H,
    initialRect: size,
  });
  const indexOf = useMemo(() => new Map(entries.map((e, i) => [e.id, i])), [entries]);
  const selectedIndex = selectedId === null ? -1 : (indexOf.get(selectedId) ?? -1);

  function onKeyDown(ev: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (ev.key === 'Enter') {
      if (selectedId !== null) {
        ev.preventDefault();
        onOpen(selectedId);
      }
      return;
    }
    const next = nextIndex(ev.key, selectedIndex, entries.length, 1, pageRows);
    const entry = next === null ? undefined : entries[next];
    if (next === null || !entry) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    onSelect(entry.id);
    virtualizer.scrollToIndex(next, { align: 'auto' });
  }

  function onClick(ev: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    const id = entryIdOf(ev.target);
    if (id !== null) onOpen(id);
  }

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      role="grid"
      aria-label={t('library.table.label')}
      aria-rowcount={entries.length + 1}
      aria-activedescendant={selectedId === null ? undefined : `entry-${selectedId}`}
      tabIndex={0}
      className="entry-table"
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <div role="row" aria-rowindex={1} className="entry-row entry-row--head">
        {COLUMNS.map((c) => (
          <div
            key={c.key}
            role="columnheader"
            aria-sort={
              sort.key === c.key ? (sort.dir === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending') : 'none'
            }
            className={`cell cell--${c.key}`}
          >
            <button type="button" className="sort-button" onClick={() => onSort(c.key)}>
              {t(c.label)}
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        {MEDIA.map((m) => (
          <div key={m} role="columnheader" className="cell cell--mark">
            {t(`art.media.${m}`)}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: 'relative' }}>
        {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map((item) => {
          const e = entries[item.index];
          if (!e) return null;
          return (
            <div
              key={e.id}
              id={`entry-${e.id}`}
              role="row"
              aria-rowindex={item.index + 2}
              aria-selected={e.id === selectedId}
              data-entry-id={e.id}
              className="entry-row"
              style={{ transform: `translateY(${item.start}px)`, height: ROW_H }}
            >
              <div role="gridcell" className="cell cell--name" title={e.name}>
                {e.name}
              </div>
              <div role="gridcell" className="cell cell--system">
                {systems[e.system]?.name ?? ''}
              </div>
              <div role="gridcell" className="cell cell--size">
                {formatSize(e.size, locale)}
              </div>
              <div role="gridcell" className="cell cell--mtime">
                {formatDate(e.mtime, locale)}
              </div>
              {MEDIA.map((m) => (
                <div key={m} role="gridcell" className="cell cell--mark">
                  <ArtStatusIcon media={m} mark={e.art[m]} />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

If `pnpm lint` reports `react-hooks/incompatible-library` on the `useVirtualizer` calls, add `// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/incompatible-library -- TanStack Virtual is not memoisable by the React Compiler` directly above each call (and only then: an unknown rule name in a disable comment is itself a lint error).

```css
.cover-grid,
.entry-table {
  height: 100%;
  overflow: auto;
  outline: none;
}

.cover-grid:focus-visible,
.entry-table:focus-visible {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--cr-accent);
}

.cover-row {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  display: flex;
  width: 100%;
  padding-top: 12px;
}

.cover-tile {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.25rem;
  padding: 0.375rem;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
  cursor: pointer;
  min-width: 0;
}

.cover-tile[aria-selected='true'] {
  border-color: var(--cr-accent);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--cr-accent) 10%, transparent);
}

.art-thumb {
  width: 100%;
  aspect-ratio: 25 / 36;
  border-radius: 0.375rem;
  object-fit: contain;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--cr-text) 6%, var(--cr-surface));
}

.art-placeholder {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 0.5rem;
  color: var(--cr-text-muted);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  text-align: center;
}

.cover-tile__name {
  overflow: hidden;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  unicode-bidi: plaintext;
}

.cover-tile__meta {
  color: var(--cr-text-muted);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
}

.cover-tile__marks {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.25rem;
}

.art-mark {
  font-size: 0.75rem;
}

.art-mark--present {
  color: #16a34a;
}

.art-mark--missing {
  color: #dc2626;
}

.art-mark--unsupported {
  color: var(--cr-text-muted);
}

.entry-row {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(12rem, 1fr) 10rem 6rem 8rem repeat(3, 5.5rem);
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--cr-border);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.entry-row[aria-selected='true'] {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--cr-accent) 12%, transparent);
}

.entry-row--head {
  position: sticky;
  z-index: 1;
  height: 34px;
  background: var(--cr-surface);
  font-weight: 600;

}

.cell {
  overflow: hidden;

  text-overflow: ellipsis;

}

.cell--size,
.cell--mark {
  text-align: right;
}

.sort-button {
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font: inherit;
  cursor: pointer;
}
```

(`unicode-bidi: plaintext` keeps a right-to-left override inside a hostile name from reordering the rest of the tile.)

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import App from '@/App';
import { I18nProvider } from '@/i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, MOCK_LIBRARIES } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { applyFilters, EMPTY_FILTERS } from '@/lib/filters';
import { dateBucket } from '@/lib/scope';
import { resetLibraryStore, useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';

const SMALL = MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path;
const EMPTY = MOCK_LIBRARIES[2].path;

async function openLibrary(path = SMALL, locale: 'en' | 'es' = 'en') {
  setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW }));
  resetLibraryStore();
  await useLibraryStore.getState().open(path);
  render(
    <I18nProvider locale={locale}>
      <App />
    </I18nProvider>,
  );
  return useLibraryStore.getState().view!;
}

describe('Library screen', () => {
  let restore: () => void;
  beforeEach(() => {
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('shows the library name and counts', async () => {
    const view = await openLibrary();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Small library' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('240 of 240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const missing = view.entries.filter((e) => e.art.cover === 'missing').length;
    expect(screen.getByText(`${missing} without cover`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('grid', { name: 'ROM covers' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('switches to a system from the Systems view', async () => {
    const view = await openLibrary();
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Views' });
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByRole('tab', { name: 'Systems' }));
    const gba = view.systems.find((s) => s.folder === 'GBA')!;
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByText(gba.name));
    expect(await screen.findByText(`${gba.roms} of 240 ROMs`)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('browses a folder from the Folders view', async () => {
    const view = await openLibrary();
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Views' });
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByRole('tab', { name: 'Folders' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByText('SFC'));
    const inSfc = view.entries.filter((e) => e.dir.startsWith('ROMS/SFC')).length;
    expect(await screen.findByText(`${inSfc} of 240 ROMs`)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('groups by date in the Date view', async () => {
    const view = await openLibrary();
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Views' });
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByRole('tab', { name: 'Date' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(nav).getByText('Older'));
    const older = view.entries.filter((e) => dateBucket(e.mtime, now) === 'older').length;
    expect(await screen.findByText(`${older} of 240 ROMs`)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('searches accent- and case-insensitively', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search by name' }), 'POKEMON edicion oro');
    expect(await screen.findByText('1 of 240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('combines filters and reflects them in counts', async () => {
    const view = await openLibrary();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }));
    const panel = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Filters' });
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('radio', { name: /^Missing cover/ }));
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('checkbox', { name: /^Super Nintendo/ }));
    const snes = view.systems.find((s) => s.folder === 'SFC')!.index;
    const expected = applyFilters(view.entries, useLibraryStore.getState().keys, {
      ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
      art: 'missing-cover',
      systems: [snes],
    }).length;
    expect(await screen.findByText(`${expected} of 240 ROMs`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters (2)' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: 'Clear filters' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('240 of 240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('toggles between grid and table', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'List' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('grid', { name: 'ROM list' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Grid' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('grid', { name: 'ROM covers' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('keeps Fill missing art disabled until M4', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Fill missing art' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('focuses the search with the slash key', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    fireEvent.keyDown(document.body, { key: '/' });
    expect(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search by name' })).toHaveFocus();
  });

  it('offers to clear when nothing matches', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search by name' }), 'zzzzqqq');
    expect(await screen.findByText('No ROMs match the search and filters.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Clear filters' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('240 of 240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('empty_library_explains_itself', async () => {
    await openLibrary(EMPTY);
    expect(screen.getByText('No ROMs found in this folder.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('speaks Spanish', async () => {
    await openLibrary(SMALL, 'es');
    expect(screen.getByText('240 de 240 ROMs')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Sistemas' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Rellenar arte que falta' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('closes back to the start screen', async () => {
    await openLibrary();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close library' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open folder…' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the components**

`src/components/library/Sidebar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { LibraryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import type { Facets } from '@/lib/filters';
import { buildFolderTree, type FolderNode } from '@/lib/folderTree';
import { DATE_BUCKETS, dateBucket, sameScope, type DateBucket, type Scope } from '@/lib/scope';
import { useViewStore } from '@/stores/viewStore';

type Tab = 'folders' | 'systems' | 'date' | 'all';
const TABS: readonly Tab[] = ['folders', 'systems', 'date', 'all'];

function tabOf(scope: Scope): Tab {
  switch (scope.kind) {
    case 'folder':
      return 'folders';
    case 'system':
      return 'systems';
    case 'date':
      return 'date';
    case 'all':
      return 'all';
  }
}

/** Folders on the single-child chain from the root start expanded. */
function initialExpanded(root: FolderNode): Set<string> {
  const open = new Set<string>();
  let node = root;
  while (node.children.length === 1) {
    const only = node.children[0]!;
    open.add(only.path);
    node = only;
  }
  return open;
}

export function Sidebar({
  view,
  facets,
  now,
}: {
  view: LibraryView;
  facets: Facets;
  now: number;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const scope = useViewStore((s) => s.scope);
  const setScope = useViewStore((s) => s.setScope);
  const setSort = useViewStore((s) => s.setSort);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(() => tabOf(scope));
  const tree = useMemo(() => buildFolderTree(view.entries), [view.entries]);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(() =>
    initialExpanded(buildFolderTree(view.entries)),
  );
  const buckets = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<DateBucket, number> = { week: 0, month: 0, year: 0, older: 0 };
    for (const e of view.entries) counts[dateBucket(e.mtime, now)] += 1;
    return counts;
  }, [view.entries, now]);

  function choose(target: Scope) {
    setScope(target);
    if (target.kind === 'date') setSort({ key: 'mtime', dir: 'desc' });
  }

  function toggle(path: string) {
    setExpanded((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(path)) next.delete(path);
      else next.add(path);
      return next;
    });
  }

  const scopeButton = (target: Scope, label: string, count: number) => (
    <button
      type="button"
      className="sidebar-item"
      aria-current={sameScope(scope, target) ? 'true' : undefined}
      onClick={() => choose(target)}
    >
      <span className="truncate" title={label}>
        {label}
      </span>
      <span className="sidebar-count">{count.toLocaleString()}</span>
    </button>
  );

  function folder(node: FolderNode, depth: number): ReactNode {
    const open = expanded.has(node.path);
    return (
      <li key={node.path}>
        <div className="sidebar-folder" style={{ paddingLeft: `${depth * 0.75}rem` }}>
          {node.children.length > 0 ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="tree-toggle"
              aria-expanded={open}
              aria-label={t(open ? 'sidebar.collapse' : 'sidebar.expand', { name: node.name })}
              onClick={() => toggle(node.path)}
            >
              {open ? '▾' : '▸'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="tree-toggle" aria-hidden="true" />
          )}
          {scopeButton({ kind: 'folder', path: node.path }, node.name, node.count)}
        </div>
        {open && node.children.length > 0 && (
          <ul className="sidebar-list">{node.children.map((c) => folder(c, depth + 1))}</ul>
        )}
      </li>
    );
  }

  return (
    <nav className="sidebar" aria-label={t('sidebar.label')}>
      <div role="tablist" aria-label={t('sidebar.label')} className="sidebar-tabs">
        {TABS.map((id) => (
          <button
            key={id}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            id={`tab-${id}`}
            aria-selected={tab === id}
            aria-controls={`panel-${id}`}
            className="sidebar-tab"
            onClick={() => {
              setTab(id);
              if (id === 'all') choose({ kind: 'all' });
            }}
          >
            {t(`views.${id}`)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div role="tabpanel" id={`panel-${tab}`} aria-labelledby={`tab-${tab}`} className="sidebar-panel">
        <ul className="sidebar-list">
          {tab === 'all' && <li>{scopeButton({ kind: 'all' }, t('views.allRoms'), view.entries.length)}</li>}
          {tab === 'systems' &&
            view.systems
              .filter((s) => s.roms > 0)
              .map((s) => (
                <li key={s.index}>
                  {scopeButton({ kind: 'system', system: s.index }, s.name, facets.systems.get(s.index) ?? 0)}
                </li>
              ))}
          {tab === 'date' &&
            DATE_BUCKETS.map((b) => (
              <li key={b}>{scopeButton({ kind: 'date', bucket: b }, t(`date.${b}`), buckets[b])}</li>
            ))}
          {tab === 'folders' && tree.children.map((n) => folder(n, 0))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/Toolbar.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { RefObject } from 'react';

import { LanguageSwitch } from '@/components/LanguageSwitch';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { LibraryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { activeFilterCount } from '@/lib/filters';
import { useFilterStore } from '@/stores/filterStore';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useViewStore } from '@/stores/viewStore';

export function Toolbar({
  view,
  searchRef,
  onSettings,
}: {
  view: LibraryView;
  searchRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  onSettings(): void;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const filters = useFilterStore((s) => s.filters);
  const setQuery = useFilterStore((s) => s.setQuery);
  const layout = useViewStore((s) => s.layout);
  const setLayout = useViewStore((s) => s.setLayout);
  const filtersOpen = useViewStore((s) => s.filtersOpen);
  const toggleFilters = useViewStore((s) => s.toggleFilters);
  const close = useLibraryStore((s) => s.close);
  const active = activeFilterCount(filters);

  return (
    <header className="toolbar">
      <div className="toolbar-title">
        <h1 className="toolbar-name truncate" title={view.root}>
          {view.name}
        </h1>
        <span className="hint">{view.profile.name}</span>
      </div>
      <input
        ref={searchRef}
        type="search"
        className="text-input toolbar-search"
        aria-label={t('toolbar.search')}
        placeholder={t('toolbar.searchPlaceholder')}
        value={filters.query}
        onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        className="ghost-button"
        aria-pressed={filtersOpen}
        aria-controls="filter-panel"
        onClick={toggleFilters}
      >
        {active > 0 ? t('toolbar.filtersActive', { n: active }) : t('toolbar.filters')}
      </button>
      <div role="group" aria-label={t('toolbar.layout')} className="segmented">
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" aria-pressed={layout === 'grid'} onClick={() => setLayout('grid')}>
          {t('toolbar.grid')}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" aria-pressed={layout === 'list'} onClick={() => setLayout('list')}>
          {t('toolbar.list')}
        </button>
      </div>
      <span className="toolbar-spacer" />
      <span title={t('toolbar.fillHint')}>
        <button type="button" className="primary-button" disabled aria-describedby="fill-hint">
          {t('toolbar.fill')}
        </button>
      </span>
      <span id="fill-hint" className="sr-only">
        {t('toolbar.fillHint')}
      </span>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={onSettings}>
        {t('start.settings')}
      </button>
      <LanguageSwitch />
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={close}>
        {t('library.close')}
      </button>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/FilterPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { REGIONS, type LibraryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import {
  ART_FILTERS,
  dayEnd,
  dayStart,
  toDateInput,
  type ArtFilter,
  type Facets,
} from '@/lib/filters';
import { useFilterStore } from '@/stores/filterStore';

const MB = 1024 * 1024;
const toMb = (bytes: number | null) => (bytes === null ? '' : String(Math.round((bytes / MB) * 100) / 100));
function toBytes(value: string): number | null {
  const n = Number.parseFloat(value);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? Math.round(n * MB) : null;
}

/** Art filters for media this layout can store. */
function artOptions(view: LibraryView): ArtFilter[] {
  const needs: Partial<Record<ArtFilter, 'cover' | 'screenshot' | 'title'>> = {
    'missing-cover': 'cover',
    'missing-screenshot': 'screenshot',
    'missing-title': 'title',
  };
  return ART_FILTERS.filter((a) => {
    const media = needs[a];
    return media === undefined || view.profile.media.includes(media);
  });
}

export function FilterPanel({ view, facets }: { view: LibraryView; facets: Facets }) {
  const t = useT();
  const f = useFilterStore((s) => s.filters);
  const setArt = useFilterStore((s) => s.setArt);
  const toggleSystem = useFilterStore((s) => s.toggleSystem);
  const toggleExt = useFilterStore((s) => s.toggleExt);
  const toggleRegion = useFilterStore((s) => s.toggleRegion);
  const setDates = useFilterStore((s) => s.setDates);
  const setSizes = useFilterStore((s) => s.setSizes);
  const clear = useFilterStore((s) => s.clear);
  const exts = useMemo(() => [...new Set(view.entries.map((e) => e.ext))].sort(), [view.entries]);
  const regions = useMemo(() => {
    const present = new Set(view.entries.flatMap((e) => e.regions));
    return REGIONS.filter((r) => present.has(r));
  }, [view.entries]);

  return (
    <section id="filter-panel" className="filter-panel" aria-label={t('toolbar.filters')}>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.art')}</legend>
        {artOptions(view).map((a) => (
          <label key={a} className="filter-option">
            <input type="radio" name="art-filter" checked={f.art === a} onChange={() => setArt(a)} />
            <span>{t(`filters.artOptions.${a}`)}</span>
            <span className="filter-count">{facets.art[a]}</span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.systems')}</legend>
        {view.systems
          .filter((s) => s.roms > 0)
          .map((s) => (
            <label key={s.index} className="filter-option">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={f.systems.includes(s.index)} onChange={() => toggleSystem(s.index)} />
              <span className="truncate">{s.name}</span>
              <span className="filter-count">{facets.systems.get(s.index) ?? 0}</span>
            </label>
          ))}
      </fieldset>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.exts')}</legend>
        {exts.map((x) => (
          <label key={x} className="filter-option">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={f.exts.includes(x)} onChange={() => toggleExt(x)} />
            <span>.{x}</span>
            <span className="filter-count">{facets.exts.get(x) ?? 0}</span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.regions')}</legend>
        {regions.map((r) => (
          <label key={r} className="filter-option">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={f.regions.includes(r)} onChange={() => toggleRegion(r)} />
            <span>{t(`regions.${r}`)}</span>
            <span className="filter-count">{facets.regions.get(r) ?? 0}</span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.date')}</legend>
        <label className="filter-field">
          {t('filters.from')}
          <input
            type="date"
            className="text-input"
            value={toDateInput(f.modifiedFrom)}
            onChange={(e) => setDates(dayStart(e.target.value), f.modifiedTo)}
          />
        </label>
        <label className="filter-field">
          {t('filters.to')}
          <input
            type="date"
            className="text-input"
            value={toDateInput(f.modifiedTo)}
            onChange={(e) => setDates(f.modifiedFrom, dayEnd(e.target.value))}
          />
        </label>
      </fieldset>
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{t('filters.size')}</legend>
        <label className="filter-field">
          {t('filters.min')}
          <input
            type="number"
            min={0}
            step="any"
            className="text-input"
            value={toMb(f.sizeMin)}
            onChange={(e) => setSizes(toBytes(e.target.value), f.sizeMax)}
          />
        </label>
        <label className="filter-field">
          {t('filters.max')}
          <input
            type="number"
            min={0}
            step="any"
            className="text-input"
            value={toMb(f.sizeMax)}
            onChange={(e) => setSizes(f.sizeMin, toBytes(e.target.value))}
          />
        </label>
      </fieldset>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={clear}>
        {t('filters.clear')}
      </button>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`src/components/library/StatusBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useContext, useMemo } from 'react';

import { LocaleContext } from '@/i18n/core';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';

export function StatusBar({
  visible,
  total,
  scanMs,
}: {
  visible: readonly EntryView[];
  total: number;
  scanMs: number;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const locale = useContext(LocaleContext);
  const n = useMemo(() => new Intl.NumberFormat(locale), [locale]);
  const missing = useMemo(() => visible.filter((e) => e.art.cover === 'missing').length, [visible]);
  return (
    <footer className="status-bar" aria-live="polite">
      <span>{t('status.shown', { shown: n.format(visible.length), total: n.format(total) })}</span>
      <span>{t('status.missingCover', { n: n.format(missing) })}</span>
      <span className="hint">{t('status.scan', { ms: n.format(scanMs) })}</span>
    </footer>
  );
}
```

Replace `src/screens/LibraryScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { LibrarySettingsDialog } from '@/components/LibrarySettingsDialog';
import { CoverGrid } from '@/components/library/CoverGrid';
import { EntryTable } from '@/components/library/EntryTable';
import { FilterPanel } from '@/components/library/FilterPanel';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/library/Sidebar';
import { StatusBar } from '@/components/library/StatusBar';
import { Toolbar } from '@/components/library/Toolbar';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { useFilterStore } from '@/stores/filterStore';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useSelectionStore } from '@/stores/selectionStore';
import { useVisibleEntries } from '@/stores/useVisibleEntries';
import { useViewStore } from '@/stores/viewStore';

function isTyping(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return (
    target instanceof HTMLInputElement ||
    target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement ||
    target instanceof HTMLSelectElement
  );
}

function Notice({ title, children }: { title: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="notice">
      <p className="notice-title">{title}</p>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export function LibraryScreen() {
  const t = useT();
  const view = useLibraryStore((s) => s.view);
  const { visible, facets, libraryFacets, now } = useVisibleEntries();
  const layout = useViewStore((s) => s.layout);
  const filtersOpen = useViewStore((s) => s.filtersOpen);
  const sort = useViewStore((s) => s.sort);
  const toggleSort = useViewStore((s) => s.toggleSort);
  const openDrawer = useViewStore((s) => s.openDrawer);
  const closeDrawer = useViewStore((s) => s.closeDrawer);
  const selectedId = useSelectionStore((s) => s.selectedId);
  const select = useSelectionStore((s) => s.select);
  const clearFilters = useFilterStore((s) => s.clear);
  const setQuery = useFilterStore((s) => s.setQuery);
  const searchRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const closeSettings = useCallback(() => setSettingsOpen(false), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    function onKey(ev: KeyboardEvent) {
      const find = ev.key === 'f' && (ev.metaKey || ev.ctrlKey);
      if (find || (ev.key === '/' && !isTyping(ev.target))) {
        ev.preventDefault();
        searchRef.current?.focus();
      } else if (ev.key === 'Escape' && !settingsOpen) {
        closeDrawer();
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [closeDrawer, settingsOpen]);

  if (!view) return null;

  const onOpen = (id: number) => {
    select(id);
    openDrawer();
  };
  const list = {
    entries: visible,
    systems: view.systems,
    generation: view.generation,
    selectedId,
    onSelect: select,
    onOpen,
  };

  let content: ReactNode;
  if (view.entries.length === 0) {
    content = (
      <Notice title={t('library.empty')}>
        <p className="hint">{t('library.emptyHint', { n: view.warnings.length })}</p>
      </Notice>
    );
  } else if (visible.length === 0) {
    content = (
      <Notice title={t('library.noMatch')}>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="ghost-button"
          onClick={() => {
            clearFilters();
            setQuery('');
          }}
        >
          {t('filters.clear')}
        </button>
      </Notice>
    );
  } else if (layout === 'grid') {
    content = <CoverGrid {...list} />;
  } else {
    content = <EntryTable {...list} sort={sort} onSort={toggleSort} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="library-shell">
      <Toolbar view={view} searchRef={searchRef} onSettings={() => setSettingsOpen(true)} />
      <div className="library-body">
        <Sidebar key={view.generation} view={view} facets={libraryFacets} now={now} />
        <main className="library-main">
          {filtersOpen && <FilterPanel view={view} facets={facets} />}
          <div className="library-content">{content}</div>
        </main>
      </div>
      <StatusBar visible={visible} total={view.entries.length} scanMs={view.scan_ms} />
      {settingsOpen && <LibrarySettingsDialog onClose={closeSettings} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import App from '@/App';
import { I18nProvider } from '@/i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, MOCK_LIBRARIES, type MockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { resetLibraryStore, useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useSelectionStore } from '@/stores/selectionStore';
import { useViewStore } from '@/stores/viewStore';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';

let backend: MockBackend;

async function setup(locale: 'en' | 'es' = 'en') {
  backend = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW });
  setBackend(backend);
  resetLibraryStore();
  await useLibraryStore.getState().open(MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path);
  render(
    <I18nProvider locale={locale}>
      <App />
    </I18nProvider>,
  );
  return useLibraryStore.getState().view!;
}

function show(entry: EntryView) {
  act(() => {
    useSelectionStore.getState().select(entry.id);
    useViewStore.getState().openDrawer();
  });
  return screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: entry.name });
}

describe('Detail drawer', () => {
  let restore: () => void;
  beforeEach(() => {
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('opens from a click on a tile with file facts', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const tile = screen.getAllByRole('gridcell')[0]!;
    await userEvent.click(tile);
    const first = view.entries.find((e) => `entry-${e.id}` === tile.id)!;
    const drawer = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: first.name });
    expect(within(drawer).getByText(first.rel_path)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText(`.${first.ext}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText(view.systems[first.system]!.name)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows a placeholder for missing art and loads present art', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const entry = view.entries.find((e) => e.art.cover === 'missing' && e.art.screenshot === 'present')!;
    const drawer = show(entry);
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('No cover')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await within(drawer).findAllByRole('presentation', { hidden: true })).not.toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('identifies on demand', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const drawer = show(view.entries[0]!);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Identify' }));
    expect(await within(drawer).findByText('Content hash')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('100%')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when identification was offline', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const drawer = show(view.entries[2]!);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Identify' }));
    expect(
      await within(drawer).findByText('Offline: identified by its file name only.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(drawer).getByText('Not found in the databases')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reveals the file and keeps the M4 actions disabled', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const entry = view.entries[5]!;
    const drawer = show(entry);
    for (const name of ['Change…', 'Search…', 'From file…', 'Remove']) {
      expect(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name })).toBeDisabled();
    }
    await userEvent.click(
      within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: /Reveal in Finder|Show in Explorer|Show in folder/ }),
    );
    expect(backend.revealed).toEqual([entry.id]);
  });

  it('drawer_renders_hostile_name_as_text', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const hostile = view.entries.find((e) => e.name === '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>')!;
    const drawer = show(hostile);
    expect(within(drawer).getByRole('heading', { name: hostile.name })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(document.querySelector('img[src="x"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('closes with Escape and with the close button', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    show(view.entries[0]!);
    fireEvent.keyDown(document.body, { key: 'Escape' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('complementary')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    const drawer = show(view.entries[0]!);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Close details' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('complementary')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('follows the keyboard selection', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    show(view.entries[0]!);
    const grid = screen.getByRole('grid', { name: 'ROM covers' });
    fireEvent.keyDown(grid, { key: 'ArrowRight' });
    expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: view.entries[1]!.name })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('speaks Spanish', async () => {
    const view = await setup('es');
    const drawer = show(view.entries[0]!);
    expect(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Identificar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cambiar…' })).toBeDisabled();
  });
});
```

(`<img alt="">` has the `presentation` role; the screenshot preview of an entry with `screenshot: 'present'` loads from the mock as an SVG blob URL.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`src/stores/identityStore.ts`:

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';

import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import type { IdentityView } from '@/ipc/types';

export type IdentityState =
  | { status: 'loading' }
  | { status: 'done'; identity: IdentityView }
  | { status: 'error'; error: BackendError };

export const identityKey = (generation: number, id: number) => `${generation}:${id}`;

interface IdentityStore {
  byKey: Record<string, IdentityState>;
  identify(generation: number, id: number): Promise<void>;
  reset(): void;
}

export const useIdentityStore = create<IdentityStore>()((set) => {
  const put = (key: string, state: IdentityState) =>
    set((s) => ({ byKey: { ...s.byKey, [key]: state } }));
  return {
    byKey: {},
    async identify(generation, id) {
      const key = identityKey(generation, id);
      put(key, { status: 'loading' });
      try {
        const backend = await getBackend();
        put(key, { status: 'done', identity: await backend.identifyEntry(generation, id) });
      } catch (e) {
        put(key, { status: 'error', error: toBackendError(e) });
      }
    },
    reset: () => set({ byKey: {} }),
  };
});
```

In `src/stores/libraryStore.ts`, import `useIdentityStore` and call `useIdentityStore.getState().reset();` next to the other resets in `open()` (success branch) and in `resetLibraryStore()`.

`src/components/library/DetailDrawer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useContext, useState } from 'react';

import { LocaleContext } from '@/i18n/core';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { errorKey, toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import { MEDIA, type EntryView, type LibraryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { formatDate, formatSize } from '@/lib/format';
import { identityKey, useIdentityStore, type IdentityState } from '@/stores/identityStore';

import { ArtThumb } from './ArtThumb';

const LATER_ACTIONS = ['change', 'search', 'fromFile', 'remove'] as const;

function revealLabel(): string {
  const ua = typeof navigator === 'undefined' ? '' : navigator.userAgent;
  if (/Mac/i.test(ua)) return 'drawer.revealMac';
  if (/Windows/i.test(ua)) return 'drawer.revealWin';
  return 'drawer.revealOther';
}

function IdentityBlock({ state }: { state: IdentityState | undefined }) {
  const t = useT();
  if (!state) return <p className="hint">{t('drawer.identifyHint')}</p>;
  if (state.status === 'loading') return <p role="status">{t('drawer.identifying')}</p>;
  if (state.status === 'error') {
    return (
      <p role="alert" className="error-banner">
        {t(errorKey(state.error.code), { detail: state.error.detail ?? '' })}
      </p>
    );
  }
  const { identity } = state;
  const pct = Math.round(identity.confidence * 100);
  return (
    <>
      <dl className="drawer-facts">
        <dt>{t('drawer.canonical')}</dt>
        <dd>{identity.canonical ?? t('drawer.notIdentified')}</dd>
        <dt>{t('drawer.methodLabel')}</dt>
        <dd>{t(`drawer.method.${identity.method}`)}</dd>
        <dt>{t('drawer.confidenceLabel')}</dt>
        <dd className="drawer-confidence">
          <meter min={0} max={100} value={pct} aria-label={t('drawer.confidenceLabel')} />
          <span>{pct}%</span>
        </dd>
      </dl>
      {identity.offline && <p className="hint">{t('drawer.offline')}</p>}
    </>
  );
}

export function DetailDrawer({
  view,
  entry,
  onClose,
}: {
  view: LibraryView;
  entry: EntryView;
  onClose(): void;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const locale = useContext(LocaleContext);
  const identity = useIdentityStore((s) => s.byKey[identityKey(view.generation, entry.id)]);
  const identify = useIdentityStore((s) => s.identify);
  const [revealError, setRevealError] = useState<BackendError | null>(null);
  const system = view.systems[entry.system];

  async function reveal() {
    setRevealError(null);
    try {
      const backend = await getBackend();
      await backend.revealEntry(view.generation, entry.id);
    } catch (e) {
      setRevealError(toBackendError(e));
    }
  }

  return (
    <aside className="drawer" aria-labelledby="drawer-title">
      <header className="drawer-header">
        <h2 id="drawer-title" className="drawer-title" title={entry.name}>
          {entry.name}
        </h2>
        <button type="button" className="icon-button" aria-label={t('drawer.close')} onClick={onClose}>
          ×
        </button>
      </header>
      <section aria-label={t('drawer.art')} className="drawer-art">
        {MEDIA.map((m) => (
          <figure key={m} className="drawer-figure">
            <ArtThumb
              generation={view.generation}
              entry={entry}
              media={m}
              className={m === 'cover' ? '' : 'art-thumb--wide'}
            />
            <figcaption>
              {t(`art.media.${m}`)} · {t(`art.mark.${entry.art[m]}`)}
            </figcaption>
          </figure>
        ))}
      </section>
      <section className="drawer-section">
        <h3 className="section-title">{t('drawer.file')}</h3>
        <dl className="drawer-facts">
          <dt>{t('drawer.path')}</dt>
          <dd className="break-all">{entry.rel_path}</dd>
          <dt>{t('columns.system')}</dt>
          <dd>{system?.name ?? ''}</dd>
          <dt>{t('drawer.ext')}</dt>
          <dd>.{entry.ext}</dd>
          <dt>{t('columns.size')}</dt>
          <dd>{formatSize(entry.size, locale)}</dd>
          <dt>{t('columns.modified')}</dt>
          <dd>{formatDate(entry.mtime, locale)}</dd>
          <dt>{t('filters.regions')}</dt>
          <dd>
            {entry.regions.length > 0
              ? entry.regions.map((r) => t(`regions.${r}`)).join(', ')
              : '—'}
          </dd>
        </dl>
      </section>
      <section className="drawer-section">
        <h3 className="section-title">{t('drawer.identity')}</h3>
        <IdentityBlock state={identity} />
        <button
          type="button"
          className="ghost-button"
          disabled={identity?.status === 'loading'}
          onClick={() => void identify(view.generation, entry.id)}
        >
          {t('drawer.identify')}
        </button>
      </section>
      <section className="drawer-section">
        <h3 className="section-title">{t('drawer.actions')}</h3>
        <div className="drawer-actions">
          {LATER_ACTIONS.map((a) => (
            <button key={a} type="button" className="ghost-button" disabled aria-describedby="drawer-later">
              {t(`drawer.${a}`)}
            </button>
          ))}
          <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void reveal()}>
            {t(revealLabel())}
          </button>
        </div>
        <p id="drawer-later" className="hint">
          {t('drawer.laterHint')}
        </p>
        {revealError && (
          <p role="alert" className="error-banner">
            {t(errorKey(revealError.code), { detail: revealError.detail ?? '' })}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

In `src/screens/LibraryScreen.tsx`: import `DetailDrawer`, read `const drawerOpen = useViewStore((s) => s.drawerOpen);` with the other hooks, compute after the `if (!view) return null;` guard `const selected = selectedId === null ? null : (view.entries[selectedId] ?? null);` (entry ids are indexes), and render after `</main>` inside `.library-body`:

```tsx
        {drawerOpen && selected && (
          <DetailDrawer view={view} entry={selected} onClose={closeDrawer} />
        )}
```

3. **Smooth scrolling, bounded DOM** — `evaluate_script`:

   ```js
   async () => {
     const grid = document.querySelector('[role="grid"]');
     const frames = [];
     let last = performance.now();
     for (let i = 0; i < 120; i += 1) {
       grid.scrollTop += 600;
       await new Promise((r) => requestAnimationFrame(r));
       const now = performance.now();
       frames.push(now - last);
       last = now;
     }
     const cells = document.querySelectorAll('[role="gridcell"]').length;
     frames.sort((a, b) => a - b);
     return {
       cells,
       scrollTop: grid.scrollTop,
       avgFrameMs: frames.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames.length,
       p95FrameMs: frames[Math.floor(frames.length * 0.95)],
     };
   }
   ```

   Expected: `cells` ≤ 120 and average frame time ≤ 20 ms (p95 ≤ 50 ms). Optionally wrap a scroll in `performance_start_trace`/`performance_stop_trace` and note the dropped frames. Screenshot `04-grid-scrolled.png`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the architecture page**

`docs/04-architecture/desktop-app.md` (English, like the other architecture pages; replace each `<…>` with the value measured in Steps 1 and 4):

```markdown
# Desktop App (Tauri shell, IPC and UI state)

> **TL;DR.** The desktop app is a thin Tauri 2 shell over `coverly-core`. Opening a library scans it once into a compact `LibraryView` that the webview browses entirely in memory (filters, facets, sort, folder tree are pure TypeScript). The backend keeps the paths: the webview asks for art, identification and "reveal" by `(generation, entry id)`, never by path. In a plain browser the same UI runs on a deterministic mock backend. Spec: [§6, §7, §8, §9 Library, §11](../superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-coverly-roms-design.md). Code: `src-tauri/src/`, `src/ipc/`, `src/stores/`, `src/lib/`, `src/components/library/`.

## Layers

| Layer | Where | Role |
|---|---|---|
| Core | `crates/coverly-core` | `scan_library_with_progress`, `fit_to_root`, `resolve_art`, `settings::LibrarySettings`, `Identifier` |
| Library layer | `src-tauri/src/library/` | `open_session` → `(Session, LibraryView)`; `read_art_bytes`, `entry_path`; `identify_entry` |
| App state | `src-tauri/src/state.rs` | generation counter, the open `Session`, `library.json` (ignored folders, recents), SQLite cache |
| Commands | `src-tauri/src/ipc.rs` | one-liners over the two layers above; blocking work on the blocking pool |
| Backend adapter | `src/ipc/` | `Backend` interface; `tauriBackend` (invoke/listen/dialog/webview) or `mockBackend` |
| Stores | `src/stores/` | zustand: library, filters, view, selection, identity, locale |
| Pure logic | `src/lib/` | search folding, filters + facets, scopes, sort, folder tree, keyboard, formatting, art URL cache |

## IPC contract

JS argument names are camelCase; payload fields are Rust snake_case. The shapes are pinned by `src/ipc/contract.sample.json`, which both `src-tauri/tests/contract.rs` and `src/ipc/contract.test.ts` check.

| Command | Arguments | Returns | Errors |
|---|---|---|---|
| `open_library` | `path`, `profileId` (null = detect) | `LibraryView` | `not-found`, `not-a-folder`, `unreadable`, `no-profile`, `unknown-profile`, `superseded` |
| `recent_libraries` | — | `RecentView[]` (with `available`) | — |
| `forget_recent_library` | `path` | `RecentView[]` | `settings` |
| `library_settings` | — | `{ ignored_dirs }` | — |
| `set_library_settings` | `ignoredDirs` | `{ ignored_dirs }` (normalised) | `settings` |
| `read_art` | `generation`, `entryId`, `media` | raw bytes (`ArrayBuffer`) | `no-library`, `stale-library`, `unknown-entry`, `art-missing`, `outside-library`, `art-too-large` |
| `identify_entry` | `generation`, `entryId` | `IdentityView` (`offline` when DATs were unreachable and uncached) | `no-library`, `stale-library`, `unknown-entry`, `io` |
| `reveal_entry` | `generation`, `entryId` | `null` | `no-library`, `stale-library`, `unknown-entry`, `outside-library`, `io` |
| `app_info` | — | `AppInfo` | — |

Event `library-scan-progress` → `{ generation, done, total, folder, roms }`: once before the first system folder, then after each.

Errors are `{ "code": "<kebab-case>", "detail"?: … }`; the UI shows `errors.<code>` from `locales/{en,es}/ui.json` (`src/ipc/errors.ts`). Codes are stable.

## Generations

Every `open_library` takes a new generation. A newer open supersedes an older one still scanning (`superseded`; the UI ignores it), and calls that name an older generation get `stale-library`. A failed open keeps the library that was already open.

## Security

- The webview never sends a path except the folder the user picked. Art and reveal resolve paths from the scan, canonicalise them and refuse anything outside the canonical library root (symlinks included) — `outside-library`. Art over 20 MiB is refused.
- No asset protocol, no `fs` plugin; capabilities are `core:default` + `dialog:allow-open`; the opener plugin is used from Rust only. CSP `img-src 'self' data: blob:`. Pinned by `src-tauri/tests/config_security.rs`.
- File names are hostile: React text only, `unicode-bidi: plaintext`, truncated with `title`; the mock's SVG escapes them. Only PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF bytes become images (`sniffImageMime`), never SVG from disk.

## Stores

| Store | State |
|---|---|
| `libraryStore` | `status` (idle/opening/ready/error), `view`, search `keys`, `path`, `progress`, `error`, `recents`; `open()` ignores stale results |
| `filterStore` | query, art status, systems, extensions, regions, date range, size range |
| `viewStore` | sidebar scope (all/system/folder/date), grid/list (remembered), sort, drawer, filter panel |
| `selectionStore` | selected entry id |
| `identityStore` | identify results per `(generation, id)` |
| `localeStore` | Auto/English/Español (localStorage until M5 settings) |

`useVisibleEntries()` derives scope → filters → sort plus the facet counts with memoised pure functions; the filter input is deferred (`useDeferredValue`) so typing stays responsive on 12k entries.

## Mock adapter

`getBackend()` picks Tauri when `window.__TAURI_INTERNALS__` exists and lazily imports `src/ipc/mock/mockBackend.ts` otherwise (its own chunk). The mock serves seeded libraries from `generateLibrary` (mulberry32): `/mock/Fixture SD (12k)` (12,000 entries, 18 systems, mixed cover/screenshot/title, hostile names, dates over 3 years), `/mock/Small library` (240), `/mock/Empty library` (0), an unavailable `/Volumes/EJECTED` recent, and `not-found` for anything else. `pickFolder()` opens the 12k fixture; dropping a folder named `X` opens `/mock/X`. Art is an escaped SVG per entry; identify answers hash for `id % 3 == 0`, offline for `id % 3 == 2`.

## Performance

| Measure | Result |
|---|---|
| Real library `~/Documents/ROMS` via `open_session` (release, cold / warm) | `<cold> / <warm>`, `<entries>` entries, `<json_bytes>` bytes of JSON |
| Generated 12,000-ROM tree (release) | `<time>` |
| 12k fixture in Chrome: grid cells mounted while scrolling | `<cells>` (budget 120) |
| 12k fixture in Chrome: frame time while scrolling (avg / p95) | `<avg> / <p95>` ms |
```
